　(a) Within W, gating events occur only at A beats (in the actual run and in the intervention reruns for every c\* ∈ \[0, z₀\] ∩ ℚ): at the B beats (29, 35, 41) the gating argument = λ·v(previous beat) + w\_{2j} + w\_{3j}; without firing v \< 1, and two rest beats precede each B beat, so λ·v(previous beat) \< λ³ = 27/64; the non-K synapses w\_{2j}, w\_{3j} are increased by potentiation only when output j fires at an A beat, and x₂ = x₃ = 0 at the start of W, so beat 26 gives no increment and beats 32 and 38 give increments (1/8)(1/8) = 1/64 and (1/8)(65/512) = 65/4096, at most 2·65/4096 per synapse in total, while at all other times the synapses receive only depression; hence w\_{2j} + w\_{3j} ≤ 1/2 + 65/1024, and the gating argument \< 27/64 + 1/2 + 65/1024 = 1009/1024 \< 1. Rest beats have no input, v only decays, and there is no firing.  
　(b) In the intervention rerun, before the first gating event, v₁ ≥ v₀ and w\_{i1} ≥ w\_{i0} (∀i) at every beat: at the start v₁(26) \> v₀(26) = 0; both paths in K equal b + c\*, and the non-K synapses are equal; depression is −A₋ d y\_j with y₀ \> y₁ = 0 (both only decay), so the synapses on the output-0 side receive no less depression than those on the output-1 side; clipping preserves order. The conclusion follows by induction and the monotonicity of the integration law.  
　(c) By (a), the first gating event occurs at some A beat; by (b), g₁ ≥ g₀ at that beat. Hence Y′ ∈ {∅, {1}, {0,1}}, each ≠ {0}; Lead₀ changes from sole lead to not leading or tied lead. ∎  
Exact results (Appendix B.5, Part 4): C\_B^first = \[0, z₀\] ∩ ℚ; on the successive pieces Y′ is ∅ (c\* \< 21993712769909167360999/365595750580871961247744 ≈ 0.06016), {1}, {0,1}, {1}, {0,1}, {1} (c\* ∈ \[4176442363370839/18014398509481984, 1/4)), {0,1} (c\* ≥ 1/4). The frozen asymmetry in states other than Sedimentation-Degree (the initial membrane potential of v₁, the residual postsynaptic trace of y₀) lets the shallow path become the sole lead after the intervention; this is a direct consequence of fragment B5, “states independent of Sedimentation-Degree … remain unchanged”. This conclusion is an instance conclusion for s1.

### 10.11 Theorems J-1 and W-1 (Share Hosts)

Let P for Family A (κ\_cons ≥ 0, 0 ≤ γΔt ≤ 1) contain only flow and rest within W (no one-sided seed dump), with no blockage; or let P for Family E (β \> 0) contain only drive and rest within W. Then:  
(1) For every valid m, I\_m is formally well-defined and unique, and after the intervention z₁ ≡ z₂ within W.  
(2) Y\_share is defined ⇔ W contains a conducting (drive) beat.  
(3) When it is defined: B\_m ⇔ κ\_eff \> 0 ∧ ∃ conducting beat t ∈ W with d(t) ≠ 0 (d given by the recursion of 4.3; κ\_eff = κ\_cons (Family A) or β (Family E)); B\_m does not depend on m.  
Proof: (1) Writing and relaxation depend only on the channel's own z and on the common ΔP (x), so equal starting values remain equal beat by beat. (2) g₀ \> 0 (w₀ \> 0), and Qᵢ \> 0 at conducting beats. (3) Q₁ − Q₂ = κ\_cons d ΔP (y₁ − y₂ = βxd); the factor 1 + k − γ ≥ 0, so d(t) always has the sign of d(t₀) or is 0 (and stays 0 once it is 0); hence Σ\_{conducting beats}(Q₁ − Q₂) ≠ 0 ⇔ κ\_eff \> 0 and d(t) ≠ 0 at some conducting beat ⇔ share ≠ 1/2; after the intervention, share = 1/2. ∎  
Program check: 12960 values for Family A and 1080 values for Family E, 0 violations (Appendix B.3, Part 2).  
Corollary (conditional D ⇒ B): for Family E under the above antecedents, D(t₀) ∧ W contains a drive beat ∧ γΔt \< 1 ⇒ B\_m^share = 1 for every valid m.  
Corollary (dynamics-level correspondence of the two families): with the parameters of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization* (g₀ = w₀ = 1, κ\_cons = β = 1, ΔP = x = 1), the expressions for Q in Family A and for y in Family E coincide term by term, and on corresponding histories the formal values of share, B\_m and F coincide item by item; the physical feasibility of I\_m differs (10.4).  
10.12 Propositions R1 and R2 (F versus Ω\_W and Bias)  
Proposition R1 (F ⇒ Ω\_W in 𝒦\_g). On 𝒦\_g hosts (bifurcation or single-synapse reading), if the habitual-travel criterion for the Familiar Route and the re-traversal criterion of Ω both take “Pass\_W ≥ N”, F and the third item of Ω use the same sub-segment partition, Pass counts opening events, and Y is the first-opened set, then F(q;T,W) ⇒ Ω\_W(q).  
Proof: F ⇒ Familiar Route ⇒ Pass ≥ N (first item of Ω); 𝒦\_g has no disconnection mechanism, so Brk ≡ 0 (second item); F ⇒ Lead\_q = sole lead on every sub-segment ⇒ q ∈ Y\_first(T\_k) ⇒ q has an opening event in W\_k (third item). ∎  
Corollary: with plasticity frozen, F and Ω can hold simultaneously with no Dτ component within W (11.6: in the fixed-weight run of s1, z on K is unchanged throughout, F = Ω = Bias = 1) — this separates F (and Ω) from the deepening action, not F from Ω. F ∧ ¬Ω requires a host with a disconnection mechanism (Family A⁺, “A4”).  
Proposition R2 (F and Bias). (1) Share reading, two-member K, Family A (κ\_cons \> 0) or Family E, every sub-segment satisfying the antecedents of Theorems J-1/W-1 (symmetric protocol, no blockage, 0 ≤ γΔt \< 1): on each sub-segment, Lead\_q = sole lead ⇔ the share of q \> 1/2 ⇔ (the sub-segment has a conducting beat and q is deeper); when q is the sole lead, the share after the intervention = 1/2, Lead becomes tied lead, and B\_m^q = 1. Hence F(q) ⇔ Familiar Route ∧ (4b) (operationalized as in 6.1), and F ∧ \[Depth\_q − Depth\_o \> ε\_δ\] ⇒ Bias. (2) F ⇏ Bias: the threshold ε\_δ of (4a) can exceed the path-depth difference (Family A, “A7”).  
Proof: (1) By the same-sign argument of Theorems J-1/W-1(3), sub-segment by sub-segment. (2) See 11.4. ∎  
10.13 Theorem R-B (B\_m and F Cannot Be Obtained from the Actual Witness History)  
There exist members G, G′ of 𝒦\_g and one declared history such that, from the initial state on, the gating, the post-update value of every synapse, v, x and y agree item by item at every beat of the whole history (so all the event witnesses of D6–D7 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* are the same), while for the two comparison tasks T = (G, K, P, W, Y, m) and T′ = (G′, K, P, W, Y, m) — which have the same comparison specification apart from M (K = K\_bif, P = the declared history of s1, W = beats 26–43, Y = first-opened set, m = max) — B\_m(T) = 1 and B\_m(T′) = 0; and for the sub-segment partition W₁, W₂, W₃ (6 beats each), F(path 0; T, W) = 1 while F(path 0; T′, W) = 0. Hence there is no function that takes the actual witness history as its only input and gives B\_m or F correctly everywhere on 𝒦\_g; the same holds for B\_m^q.  
Proof: G = G\_SNN (θ₀ = θ₁ = 1); G′ changes only θ₁, to θ₁ = 3 (Definition 1 of 𝒦\_g allows different θ\_j for different outputs). In the declared history of s1, output 1 fires in neither model (v₁ \< 1 throughout), θ₁ enters no actual event, and the whole histories agree item by item. Whole window: after I\_max the path sum of output 1 is ≈ 1.337; in G it opens already at beat 26, Y′ = {0,1}, B = 1; in G′ each entry drive is at most ≈ 1.337 and occurs at most once every three beats, so v₁ ≤ 1.337/(1 − 27/64) ≈ 2.31 \< 3; output 1 does not open, Y′ = {0}, B = 0. Sub-segment by sub-segment: the two models have the same actual states at the starts of the three sub-segments; in every sub-segment the original reading is Lead₀ = sole lead; in G, B^q is true in every sub-segment (both for m = max and for all valid m); in G′, in every sub-segment output 1 still does not reach θ₁ = 3 after I\_max, Lead₀ is unchanged, and B^q = 0. The Familiar-Route conditions of the two models are the same. Hence F(T) = 1 and F(T′) = 0. If some function f were correct everywhere, f would take two values on the same input, a contradiction. ∎ Program (Appendix B.5, Part 9): whole histories identical: True; by sub-segment, G gives (sole lead, true, true)×3 and G′ gives (sole lead, false, false)×3.  
Corollary (representation requirements): D is a function of the state at the intervention time; Ω\_W is a function of Pass\_W, Brk\_W and the passage events of each sub-segment in the actual witness history; Bias (as operationalized in 6.1) is a function of the state and the actual pass counts; the Familiar Route is a function of the state and Pass\_W. B\_m and B\_m^q require the transition relation of M, the state at the intervention time, P, W, Y, m and the write-back rule (under aggregation, R-uniform and R-shift can give different B for the same M and the same c\*, 11.1); F requires the Familiar-Route information and the information needed for one B\_m^q per sub-segment. Hence B\_m and F cannot be defined from D, Ω\_W, Bias, the Familiar Route or any actual-history quantity — this is the common ground, at the representation level, of the various “⇏” of 11.4. It is a representation-level conclusion, not a conclusion about observational distinguishability: the actual witness history alone does not suffice to determine B\_m and F; the information added must suffice to constrain the counterfactual transitions, and it may come from valid intervention or from certified mechanism or model information (in the example above, the certified program parameter θ₁ distinguishes G from G′ without any state intervention on the host); whether intervention is necessary under a given declared observation set must be proved separately for that observation set.  
10.14 Proposition DG-1 (Potentiation Equals Depression)  
In 𝒦\_g, let A₊ = A₋, d\_x,i = d\_y,j and ρ = 0; if in the declared history sᵢ(t) = g\_j(t) always holds for synapse (i, j) (pre and post strictly in the same beat), xᵢ and y\_j have equal initial values, and there is no clipping, then w\_ij is constant.  
Proof: x′ᵢ and y′\_j satisfy the same recursion with equal initial values, so they are equal beat by beat; in a beat where both occur, P⁺ + P⁻ = A₊x′ᵢ − A₋y′\_j = 0, and they never occur in different beats. ∎  
In the formation history of s1 the teaching current makes output 0 fire exactly at the A beats, so strict same-beat timing holds: with A₊ = A₋ = 1/16, after formation z\_T = (0, 0), the path depths of the bifurcation K are (0, 0), and D = 0; by Proposition B-D(1) (both paths uniform within, R-shift equal-depth identity) and F ⇒ B, B\_m and F are both 0 (Appendix B.5, Part 10; all 300 random strictly same-beat histories stay at baseline). This is a net-zero degeneration of the deepening action Dτ, not a degeneration of I\_m.

Section 11  
Counterexamples and Boundary Witnesses  
All the following constructions lie within the working input of Section 2, and each corresponds to a reading in Appendix B.  
11.1 Failure of B ⇒ D and Reading Equivalence under the Auxiliary Operation R-uniform (Appendix B.6, Parts 3 and 7)  
Two entries and two outputs, λ = 0, θ = 1/4, d\_x = d\_y = 1, A₊ = 0, A₋ = 1/2, ρ = 0, b = 1/4, \[lo, hi\] = \[0, 2\]; weights by entry rows ((0, 1/2), (1, 1/2)), v = x = 0, y = (1, 1); both paths have mean depth 1/4, D = 0, c\* = 1/4; protocol (A, teaching (−2, −2)), (A, (0, 0)). Actual gating (0,0), (1,0), first-opened set {0}; after R-uniform all four weights are 1/2, gating (0,0), (0,0), first-opened set ∅, B = 1. Mechanism: the common depression of 1/2 at the first beat is cut off by lower clipping at w00 = 0 (pre-clip sum −1/2), while the other weight keeps 1/2; after the uniform write-back both paths drop together to 0 — clipping turns the within-path difference into a difference of path sums. In the same example R-shift writes back the identity, and B = 0. Among 1689 random states uneven within paths but of equal depth, B ⇒ D fails in 5 cases under R-uniform, all accompanied by clipping (0 failures where neither run has clipping, consistent with Proposition B-D(3)); under R-shift, 0 failures.  
Reading-equivalence example (Appendix B.6, Part 7): two entries and two outputs, synchronous entries, fixed weights (plastic off), equal-depth weights by entry rows ((1/4, 1/2), (3/4, 1/2)), both path sums 1; R-uniform changes all four weights to 1/2, which changes the state, yet the entire gating record is the same. This shows that equal-depth identity is sufficient but not necessary for preserving B ⇒ D; reading equivalence of a non-identity operation does not change the upstream definitional requirement on valid write-back.  
11.2 Disagreement between Reading Types (Appendix B.5, Parts 4 and 6)  
(A) Order-type true, share false: bifurcation, plasticity off, S₀ = 3/5, S₁ = 11/20 (split equally between the two synapses of each path), non-K synapses 1/4, v = y = 0, P = A×3: original Y\_first = {0}, share (1/2, 1/2); for every c\* ∈ \[1/40, 1/20\] ∩ ℚ, Y′\_first = {0,1} (B^first = B^order = 1), while the share remains (1/2, 1/2) (B^share = 0).  
(B) Share true, order-type false: bifurcation, plasticity off, S₀ = 1, S₁ = 3/5, non-K synapses 1/4, P = AB, A×5: original Y\_first = {0,1}, share (2/3, 1/3); for every c\* ∈ \[1/20, 1/4\] ∩ ℚ the share becomes (1/2, 1/2) (B^share = 1), while the first-opened set and the order of precedence are unchanged.  
(C) The s1 instance: single-synapse K, m = max: share (1, 0) → (3/5, 2/5), the order of precedence changes (output 1 goes from not opening to opening), the first-opened set {0} is unchanged: B^share = B^order = 1, B^first = 0.  
Both witnesses (A) and (B) satisfy SYM, so the agreement of Theorem TH-3 does not extend to 𝒦\_g.  
11.3 Sources of D Holding while B\_m Fails  
Under the antecedents of Theorem S-FORK (by its (1)), D ∧ ¬B^first ⇔ D ∧ \[Y = {0,1} ∨ Y = ∅\], which is exhaustive within the antecedents. Outside the antecedents, each source on the hosts has an exact witness:  
　(α) Law level: the dependence of Y on Depth\_K is zero for every value of the frozen quantities — Family A with κ\_cons = 0 (Appendix B.3, Part 3: B = 0; 0 for every brk ∈ {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0)}). 𝒦\_g has no case of this kind: the gating argument reads w\_ij with coefficient 1, so as soon as P activates the entries of K, the Sedimentation-Degree enters the gating.  
　(β) State level: some value of the frozen quantities makes B true, but the actual value suppresses it — Family A⁺ with channel 2 blocked (aug): B(min, mean, max) = (0, 0, 0); once the blockage is lifted, B\_mean = 1 (a different counterfactual on the frozen quantities, not I\_m, used only to separate (α) from (β)). The blocked instance of the two-path threshold model (first branch of Theorem TH-2) belongs to the same class. On Host A, Brk ≡ 0, and there is no case of this kind.  
　(γ) Resolution level: in discrete time both thresholds are crossed in the same beat (Appendix B.2, Part 5: Depth = (2, 1), p = (0, 1), Y = {a, b}, B = 0; with p changed to (0, 2/5, 1), B = 1).  
　(δ) Window level: p does not reach g(c\*) within W (Theorem TH-2).  
　(ε) Level of frozen non-K asymmetry: s1 single-synapse K, m = min (w10 \> w11 keeps output 0 in the lead as before).  
　(ζ) Protocol-timing level: the s1 convergence reading (entries in different beats).  
　(η) Relaxation-erasure level: with ρ = 1, plastic on (bap irrelevant) and the entry closed at the first beat of W, relaxation erases the path-depth difference at the first beat (endpoint in 10.9); within the antecedents of Theorem S-FORK this falls under the branch “Y = {0,1} or ∅”.  
κ\_cons = 0 and channel blockage give the same combination of truth values on T (D = 1, B = 0) but belong to different classes: in (α) B is 0 for every value of the frozen quantities, in (β) it is not. Whether the classification outside the antecedents is exhaustive is unproved (U12-3).  
11.4 Relations among D, B\_m, F, Ω\_W and Bias (Each “⇒” with Its Conditions, Each “⇏” with a Witness)  
　F ⇒ B\_m^q ⇒ B\_m: fragment B7. B\_m ⇒ D: holds in general at the path level or under a valid write-back rule (equal-depth identity, R-shift) (Proposition B-D(1)); the auxiliary reading of the auxiliary operation R-uniform satisfies it under the antecedents of Theorem S-INV or S-FORK, and it can fail under clipping (11.1). No violation occurs in the computed cases listed for the four hosts.  
　D ⇏ B\_m: (α)–(η) of 11.3. Conditional D ⇒ B: Family E (corollary of Theorem W-1); the two-path threshold model and Host A, under the antecedents of Theorems TH-1 and S-FORK respectively, when a P₁₇-type condition holds.  
　B\_m ⇏ F: s1 single input (A followed by 17 rest beats: B^first true for every valid m, F = 0, Ω = 0, Bias = 0); Family A “A6” (flow followed by rest×5: B = 1, F = 0).  
　F ⇏ Ω\_W: Family A⁺ “A4” (aug: channel 1 cut off at the last beat, F = 1, Ω = 0). F ⇒ Ω\_W: under the aligned declarations of 𝒦\_g (Proposition R1).  
　Ω\_W ⇏ F: the s1 equal-depth state (taking the state after applying I\_max at beat 26 as the starting point: D = 0, F = 0, Ω = 1, each path opens 3 times); Family A “A2” (κ\_cons = 0: Ω = 1, F = 0) and “A5” (equal depth).  
　Ω\_W ⇏ D, B\_m, Bias: the same two examples. D ⇏ Ω\_W: “A6”.  
　Bias ⇒ D: (4a) gives a difference \> ε\_δ ≥ 0. D ⇏ Bias: “A7” ((4a) fails); single input ((4b) fails).  
　Bias ⇏ B\_m: s1 single-synapse K ((4a)(4b) hold, and at c\* = min B is 0 under all three readings); Family A⁺ “A3” (channel 2 blocked: Bias = 1, B = 0). Bias ⇏ F: the same two examples.  
　B\_m ⇏ Bias: single input, “A6”.  
　F ∧ ε\_δ threshold ⇒ Bias: share hosts (Proposition R2(1)); under the first-opened reading, the relation between F and the counting operationalization of (4b) is unproved (U12-2).  
　Bias ⇏ Ω\_W: “A4” (Bias = 1, Ω = 0). Ω\_W ⇏ Bias: the equal-depth state (Ω = 1, Bias = 0).  
　Familiar Route and Bias (fragment C1, “This chapter does not establish the relation between the two”): Familiar Route ∧ ¬Bias (equal-depth state, A2, A5); Bias ∧ Familiar Route ∧ ¬F (A3); no implication between the two is established.  
　“A Familiar Route does not presuppose Ω” (fragment A6): Familiar Route = 1 while Ω = 0 (A4, aug).  
Case table for Family A (Appendix B.3, Part 4; m = mean, 3 sub-segments, ε\_δ = 1/100, q = channel 1): A1 baseline (κ\_cons = 1, z = (2, 0), flow×6): D = B = F = Ω = Bias = 1; A2 κ\_cons = 0: D = 1, B = F = 0, Ω = 1, Bias = 0; A3 channel 2 blocked for the whole window (aug): D = 1, B = F = 0, Ω = Bias = 1; A4 channel 1 cut off at the last beat (aug): D = B = F = 1, Ω = 0, Bias = 1; A5 equal depth z = (1, 1): D = B = F = 0, Ω = 1, Bias = 0; A6 single conduction: D = B = 1, F = Ω = Bias = 0; A7 z = (1/200, 0): D = B = F = Ω = 1, Bias = 0.

### 11.5 Degeneration Mechanisms

(1) Potentiation = depression: Proposition DG-1.  
(2) Clipping and δmax: I\_m at the path level and the auxiliary operation R-uniform take only intermediate values and need no clipping; under R-shift, validity is decided by C\_leg. δmax corresponds to a constant upper bound (6.1). Saturation makes D vanish within W: with bifurcation starting path depths (6/5, 9/10), after A×30 (plasticity on) both paths reach 7/4 and D vanishes; on this window the first-opened B is false for every valid m (both paths open in the same beat from the outset) (Appendix B.5, Part 10). Under sustained strengthening, once both paths saturate, D and B^q vanish on the later sub-segments and F cannot persist — clipping is one failure mechanism of F. Clipping also destroys the conclusion of Theorem S-INV (witness in 10.3) and the antecedent NC of Theorem S-FORK.  
(3) Duty assignment of the depression component: by Definition 5 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*, the read set of a depression event is gr\_j ∪ {y\_j}, with gr\_j ∋ w\_ij (when sᵢ = 1); in the same beat y′\_j = d y\_j + g\_j, and g\_j is determined by w through gating, so the depression component reads the weights stepwise via gating, not proportionally. Witness: changing only w00 at beat 26 (z = 0 and z ≈ 0.418), the depression component for input 0 → output 0 is −37449/1073741824 and −67146313/1073741824 respectively (Appendix B.5, Part 10). Assignment: depression is triggered by presynaptic firing (continuation passing) and its amplitude depends on the postsynaptic trace; it is not the Structural Position's own relaxation; relaxation (−ρ(w − b), which reads the depth of the position itself) is a candidate of the field-intrinsic shallowing class. The original text of the definition of Eλ in Equation 2 is not among the fragments; following 6.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization*, “components whose mechanism attribution cannot be determined are recorded as undetermined and not forcibly assigned”, the depression component is recorded as undetermined (candidate R\_other). D, B\_m and F are evaluated by rerunning all events in the R layer and are not affected by this booking.  
(4) Undefined reading: total share 0 (s1 bifurcation and convergence, for c\* below about 0.06016 and 0.04071 respectively).  
(5) P does not activate the entries of K: the gating does not read K, and B is false or the reading is undefined.  
11.6 Instance Results for s1 (Appendix B.5)  
At every intervention point of s1 (beats 26, 32, 38) the paths are internally uniform, R-uniform and R-shift agree item by item in all pieces and values, and C\_leg = \[min, max\] ∩ ℚ. All of the following are instance conclusions for s1.  
　Bifurcation: first-opened set and order of precedence true for every valid m (Theorem S-S1); share reading undefined for c\* \< ≈0.06016 and true otherwise.  
　Convergence (share only): reading undefined for c\* \< ≈0.04071; false for c\* ∈ \[≈0.04300, ≈0.04853) ∪ \[≈0.06255, ≈0.10165), true otherwise — under the same gating unit and the same threshold mechanism B\_m varies with m, the source being the asymmetric entry timing (in each cycle A precedes B), i.e. a consequence of the failure of C₌ (i)(ii).  
　Single synapse: first-opened set false for every valid m; share and order of precedence true for c\* ≥ ≈0.12032 — the non-K asymmetry (w10 \> w11) means the threshold mechanism is not the same, and B\_m varies with m (an instance on Host A of the last sentence of fragment B5).  
　“Resetting all weights to baseline at the start of W” coincides component by component with I\_min (K\_bif, K\_conv): at beat 26 all non-K synapses are at baseline, and the shallow path in K has depth 0 = min\_K Depth. In the general case (min\_K Depth \> 0 or non-K synapses off baseline) a reset is not I\_m. Hence on s1 the Trace-Response-erasing intervention can be read as I\_min (K and m = min must be declared): the bifurcation first-opened set goes {0} → ∅, B\_min = 1; under the share reading there is no firing after the intervention, and the reading is undefined. Cutting off backpropagation and freezing plasticity do not change z on K at the intervention time; they change only the process law and are not I\_m.  
　F, Ω\_W, Bias (bifurcation, q = path 0, 3 sub-segments × 6 beats, ε\_δ = 1/100): both the actual run and the fixed-weight run have D = F = Ω = Bias = 1 and Familiar Route = 1, with opening events (3, 0); in the fixed-weight run z on K is unchanged throughout. Single-synapse K: D = 1, F = 0, Bias = 1. Single input: D = 1, B = 1, F = 0, Ω = 0, Bias = 0.  
11.7 Boundary Witnesses for the Valid Parameter Domain (Appendix B.6, Part 5)  
Degenerate case: w00 = 0, w10 = 2, all others 1/4, bifurcation K path depths (3/4, 0): C\_leg = {3/4}; I\_c is undefined for c = 0, 1/4, 1/2 and defined for c = 3/4; m = min is valid as a function but I\_m is undefined, while m = max is defined. Non-degenerate case: w00 = 1/2, w10 = 3/2: C\_leg = \[1/4, 3/4\]. Shared Structural Positions: K = {path A, input-0 branch} is rejected under R-shift, and the parameter machine gives undef-Im on the whole range. Among 400 random states uneven within paths, 147 have C\_leg properly contained in \[min, max\] — on these states conclusions “for every valid m” do not hold, and claims can be made only within C\_leg.

Section 12  
Breach  
This system is breached if any one of the following holds.  
K1  Internal contradiction: under the same contract, I\_m is judged defined while the write-back is not unique; or the parameter machine gives a gating record or a true or false B outside C\_leg.  
K2  Read-back conflict: under the antecedents of 8.2 (the value taken from a valid write-back rule, or transferred through reading equivalence), B\_m = 1 in M while all members on K have equal Sedimentation-Degree (conflicting with Proposition 1 of fragment B6); F = 1 while q does not satisfy fragments A6 and C1; the result of a reset to baseline is read back as I\_m for B when min\_K Depth \> 0; an auxiliary reading is read back as the upstream B when reading equivalence has not been established; the share reading is read back as the precedence of Law 5; the entry drive u\_A^{ρ\_eff} is taken as the shared entry flow pressure of C₌, or Theorem 17 is invoked when C₌ fails and no formally equivalent representation exists.  
K3  Omission of a load-bearing symbol from the correspondence table: m, the write-back rule, the valid domain, the reading type, the freeze list, the switches (plastic, bap) or the grade of the path threshold rule is omitted in a way that affects the value of B\_m.  
K4  Mismatch of relation properties: B\_m^q and B\_m are declared equivalent; “more familiar” is declared a total order without equal depth being handled; the actual properties of the reading-coarsening relation disagree with R-6.  
K5  Compressed evaluation: an evaluation that takes only the actual witness history as input gives B\_m or F correctly everywhere on 𝒦\_g (contrary to Theorem R-B).  
K6  Quantifier overreach: inferring “physical intervention is the only necessary protocol component” from “the actual witness history is insufficient”; issuing true or false “for every valid m” when \[min, max\] ∩ ℚ ⊄ C\_leg; writing an instance property of s1 as a general fact about 𝒦\_g; computing the path-depth difference with plasticity off using ρ in place of ρ\_eff.  
The following do not constitute a breach: I\_m undefined on some host (sum aggregation over nested paths of unequal length; c\* ∉ C\_leg with R-shift leaving the box; R-shift rejecting on shared Structural Positions) — recorded as undef-Im, with no true or false B, and not meaning that every write-back is impossible at that c*; I\_m physically infeasible (Family E outside the resource bound or with no declared setting route; Family A within the current declaration); D = 0 and B = 1 obtained with the auxiliary operation R-uniform where reading equivalence has not been established — an auxiliary reading is not the value of a valid write-back (S8) and is not taken as a reading; B\_m varying with m (reported as C\_B); an undefined share reading; the numerical coincidence of reset and I\_min on s1 (with K and m declared); SYM failing on s1, with the verdict reached by direct check and Theorem S-S1; no order-type reading under the convergence reading; the depression component recorded as undetermined in assignment; Proposition R2 under the first-opened reading and exhaustiveness outside the antecedents of 11.3 being unproved; read-back antecedents not holding; no measurements on real hosts; minimality and completeness of *The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics\* being unproved.

Section 13  
Conditional Items and Status

### 13.1 Load-Bearing Conditions

The propositions and theorems of Section 10 hold under the listed antecedents, all of which are written into the hypotheses of the respective theorems: SYM, ENT, P-A, NC (Theorem S-FORK), NC′ and c ∈ C\_leg (its Corollary 1); paths that are entry groups, mutually non-overlapping paths, only K changed with all other starting states the same, ENT, equal x within a group, no clipping (Theorem S-INV, Proposition B-D(3)); no clipping in K (Lemma C-REP); C\_leg and “\[min, max\] ∩ ℚ ⊆ C\_leg” (for universal statements); symmetric protocol, no blockage and the parameter domain (Theorems J-1, W-1); the R-pool rule (Theorem W-2). There are no load-bearing H entries. Reading the structures back onto the hosts requires the antecedents of Section 8.

### 13.2 Three Kinds of Strength

Model conclusions: all theorems of Section 10 and all witnesses of Section 11, limited to the four declared hosts, the listed family-level antecedents and the contract; the conclusions on Host A belong to a program host (algorithmic level) and imply nothing about neuromorphic hardware or biological neural tissue; the physical conclusions on Families A and E hold only within the idealizations and constitutive antecedents of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization*; the conclusions on s1 are instance conclusions.  
Conditional inference for real hosts: planing off deposits or pre-cutting channels in a riverbed flume constitutes I\_m if the intermediacy and freezing conditions are met (the deposits bear the Sedimentation-Degree of the paths, the amount planed off brings all paths to a common level, and discharge and blockage states are unchanged); otherwise it reads only as sedimentary-configuration dependence (dependence on a change of common level, not B). Independent premises: a declaration of the host bearer of riverbed Sedimentation-Degree and of the path-aggregation scheme, and a physical route for the “removal” operation. Breach mode: in a flume that meets the premises, report each reading type separately after planing; a disagreement between the first-avulsion reading and the share reading does not constitute a breach, and the two must be reported separately as in 10.7. None of the listed premises has been verified.  
Empirical certification: none.  
The three columns do not substitute for one another: B\_m = 1 and F = 1 within the model are not written up as certified for a real host; the lack of an existing experiment is not grounds for a verdict of 0.

### 13.3 Open Items

　U12-1  Correspondence of upstream clauses (the cited fragments and formulations follow the text in main book v0.7.8, 2.2): the correspondence of Definition 19 (Sedimentary Saturation) of *The Mathematical Form of the Field* with clipping, and of Eλ in Equation 2 with the depression component, is not established.  
　U12-2  The relation between F and the counting operationalization of Bias (4b) under the first-opened reading on Host A.  
　U12-3  Whether the classification of the sources of D ∧ ¬B is exhaustive outside the antecedents of Theorem S-FORK.  
　U12-4  The number of pieces of the parameter machine has only a coarse bound; solvability for stochastic and continuous input families.  
　U12-5  Physical declaration of the out-of-boundary setting operations required by I\_m for Families A and E; the “removal” process of Family A has no declared physical route.  
　U12-6  The state-dependent part of δmax, and the correspondence of positions with z \< 0 in *The Mathematical Form of the Field*.  
　U12-7  Whether B\_m is independent of m for the convergence reading on Host A when the entry timing is symmetrized (rotating the alternation order of A and B).  
　U12-8  A write-back rule with equal-depth identity when paths share Structural Positions (R-shift rejects); its existence and uniqueness.  
　U12-9  When C\_leg is properly contained in \[min, max\], whether another rule with equal-depth identity can enlarge the range of m for which I\_m is defined.  
　U12-10  The p̂ of Lemma C-REP is derived from the gating dynamics; its mechanism correspondence as the host's entry flow pressure is not established, and the mechanism-level read-back of full C₌ remains to be established.  
　U12-11  Extension of Theorem S-INV to overlapping entry groups: it must be proved separately that the total weight of each actual activation set is determined by the path-sum constraints.

Section 14  
Interface

### 14.1 Inputs

*The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics* v1.2: Definitions 4 to 6; Sections 1, 4, 5 and 6 of Appendix 1 (fragments B1–B8).  
*The Mathematical Form of the Field* v2.7: Definitions 2, 3, 4, 5, 9, 11, 12, 13, 14 and 22; Equations 2, 3a, 4 and 11; Law 5; Theorem 17 (fragments A0–A9).  
*Familiar Route Measure Equations* v1.2: Sections 3 and 11 (fragments C1–C2).  
*The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0: the R and F layers, D4–D21, Theorems 1 and 3, 6.1, 8.2–8.6.  
*The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0: Definitions 1–7 and Proposition 1 of Section 3; 3.3.  
*The Construction Form of Field Concretization* v1.0: 4.2 and 4.3 of Section 4, C-C, Lemma 1, Proposition 5.

### 14.2 Outputs

To *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts*: the structural-quantity column for Host A — realization and rerunning of I\_m on that paper's declared R-layer instance (Appendix A.8 loads that paper's appendix program A.2 directly); I\_m and 𝒟₁ are different intervention domains (6.4).  
To *The Mathematical Form of Duty Correspondence in Field Concretization* (transfer between the formal layer and the physical layer): Families A and E have identical B\_m and F values item by item at the formal layer but differ at the physical layer in the feasibility of I\_m (corollaries of Theorems J-1 and W-1, 10.4); they can serve as an instance pair for listing the formal and physical layers separately and for the boundary at which correspondence fails. The bifurcation reading of Host A is of the same type as Family E but has different reading-type conclusions (disagreement between the share and order-type readings arises only on 𝒦\_g, which has reset thresholds).  
To *The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics*: D, B\_m, B\_m^q, F, Ω\_W and Bias with the definitions and correspondences of Sections 3, 6 and 9 (formal definitions of deepening, Familiar Route, Continuation Bias and Structural Bias); the pieces of c\* ↦ Y on the four hosts (Theorem S-PAR) and the criteria of Theorems TH-1, S-FORK, J-1 and W-1; Theorem R-B (the actual witness history alone does not suffice to determine B; a protocol must add information sufficient to constrain the counterfactual transitions, which may come from valid intervention or from certified mechanism or model information; whether intervention is necessary under a particular observation set is to be proved separately). Observation protocols are not given by this system.  
To *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits* and *The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields* (biological neural and riverbed hosts): the four-category classification of I\_m and its discriminating conditions (10.5); the reading-type rules (10.7: the type is a load-bearing symbol; the share requires a domain of definition; the conditions for TH-3-type agreement on hosts with one-time opening); the classification of the sources of D ∧ ¬B (11.3); on physical hosts I\_m requires a declared out-of-boundary setting operation (for instance, planing off deposits in a riverbed is a “removal” operation, which must meet intermediacy and freezing, and otherwise reads only as sedimentary-configuration dependence). The concrete correspondence of structural quantities under the river-channel reading belongs to the adjudication of the riverbed host and is not given by this system.  
To *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Artificial Neural Networks* (spiking-neural-network host): the exact results of the three readings of s1, Theorems S-S1 and S-FORK, and the parameter machine.  
To Field Induction: I\_m on Host A can serve as a candidate element of licensed intervention; its qualification as licensed intervention is not given by this system.  
To *The Necessity Form of Field-Concretization Qualification*: the definition sequence of this system is cited only when that paper's conditions are stated through field structural quantities.

## Appendix A  Program Source

The following 9 programs run in the same directory; they use only the Python standard library, with no third-party dependencies, and all numerical values are computed exactly with fractions.Fraction. A.1 and A.2 are frozen dependencies, whose SHA-256 is checked by A.3 at load time; A.3 and A.4 are only imported; A.5–A.9 are the run entry points (A.8 is also imported by A.9). The programs give finite verification and exact witnesses; random generation covers only its generating domain, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.  
A.1  snn\_core.py  Program of the frozen SNN instance s1 (only loaded and checked; its SHA-256 is checked before loading)

~~~python
"""Frozen SNN instance s1: a replayable SNN instance (program host). Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library, deterministic, replayable event by event.
Host instance χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W):
  P this program; L the algorithmic level (discrete time steps); B = {4 input neurons, 2 output LIF neurons, 8 synapses}; W the declared window.
  Input-neuron firing is fixed by an external stimulus sequence (out-of-boundary signal); the teaching current teach is injected into output 0 only during the formation phase (out-of-boundary signal).
  Output neurons: LIF, v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext; when v ≥ θ the neuron fires and resets to 0.
  Plasticity: pair-based STDP (trace form): presynaptic traces x_i and postsynaptic traces y_j are multiplied by DEC each step and incremented by 1 on firing;
    when postsynaptic neuron j fires (via the backpropagated signal reaching the synapse), w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i; when presynaptic neuron i fires, w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j;
    weights are clipped to [0, W_MAX]. Optional relaxation toward baseline (RELAX, default 0).
  Intervention switches: plastic (plasticity on/off); bap (whether postsynaptic firing propagates back to the synapse; when off, STDP does not see postsynaptic firing, i.e. the route is cut).
Trace-Response: above-baseline weight z_ij = w_ij − W0.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """One step: returns (new state, event record). The event record lists presynaptic firing, postsynaptic firing and the plasticity components of each synapse (positive, negative, relaxation)."""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist: [(input name, teaching current)]. Returns (final state, event log)."""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """Fingerprint of the frozen log: SHA-256 of the events serialized in a fixed order."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.2  gate\_class.py  Verbatim identical to Appendix A.2 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0: Definitions 1–3 of 𝒦\_g and Proposition 1 (the R-layer representation of Host A, 2.4)

~~~python
"""Independent implementation of the formal definition of the event-gating class 𝒦_g (main text 3.1, Definitions 1–3), and beat-by-beat finite verification that “the frozen SNN is a member of 𝒦_g” (main text, Proposition 1).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 gate_class.py
The frozen snn_core.py (Appendix A.1) is loaded only as the object under check; its SHA-256 is verified before loading.
Parameters of a K_g member G (Definition 1):
  n_in, n_out          number of entry units, number of gating units; Structural Positions X = {(i, j)} (fully connected)
  pat: Σ_in -> {0,1}^n_in  entry letter to entry pulse vector (including rest 'none' -> zero vector)
  lam[j], theta[j], vreset[j]   leak, threshold, reset value
  dx[i], dy[j]         eligibility-trace decay
  Ap, Am, rho          potentiation amplitude, depression amplitude, relaxation rate
  b[i][j], lo[i][j], hi[i][j]   baseline, valid interval
Protocol switches (declared items, not part of the state): plastic, bap
Letter σ = (entry name, I vector); I is the out-of-boundary injection (teaching current).
"""
import sys, hashlib, random, itertools, importlib.util
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
SNN_DIR = __import__('os').path.dirname(__import__('os').path.abspath(__file__))   # the frozen snn_core.py sits in the same directory as this program (Appendix A.1)
FROZEN_SHA = {'snn_core.py': '4e0fd45d2442890ecf3a7687ea285f89ed161e6fb4ab1083ca0fb79821960e49'}

class Gate:
    """A member of K_g (Definition 1). State o = (w, v, x, y); w[i][j] are the Structural Positions, v, x, y the auxiliary positions."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, vreset, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
        self.n_in, self.n_out, self.pat = n_in, n_out, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta, self.vreset = list(lam), list(theta), list(vreset)
        self.dx, self.dy = list(dx), list(dy)
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.rho = Ap, Am, rho
        self.b, self.lo, self.hi = b, lo, hi
        self.check_domain()
    def check_domain(self):
        """Parameter constraints of Definition 1; anything that violates them is not a K_g member."""
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
        assert all(len(p) == self.n_in and all(c in (0, 1) for c in p) for p in self.pat.values())
        assert all(0 <= l <= 1 for l in self.lam) and all(0 <= d <= 1 for d in self.dx + self.dy)
        assert 0 <= self.rho <= 1 and self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                assert self.lo[i][j] <= self.b[i][j] <= self.hi[i][j]
        return True
    # ---------- Definition 2: per-beat execution order (eight stages, reading the pre-beat state) ----------
    def step(self, o, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
        """Returns (o', rec). rec lists the gate arguments vt, g, and for each synapse (P+, P-, Pr, pre-clip sum, post-clip value)."""
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sigma
        s = self.pat[name]                                                     # (1) read input
        vt = [self.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)) + I[j]
              for j in range(self.n_out)]                                      # (2) integrate (pre-beat w)
        g = [1 if vt[j] >= self.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (3) gate, comparison >=
        v2 = [self.vreset[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (4) reset
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]              # (5) eligibility traces
        y2 = [self.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        w2 = [[None] * self.n_out for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        comp = {}
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                pp = self.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)   # (6) potentiation: only if g_j = 1
                pm = -self.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)  #     depression: only if s_i = 1
                pr = -self.rho * (w[i][j] - self.b[i][j]) if (plastic and self.rho != 0) else F(0)
                S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
                nw = min(self.hi[i][j], max(self.lo[i][j], S))                 # (7) clipping
                w2[i][j] = nw
                comp[(i, j)] = (pp, pm, pr, S, nw)
        rec = dict(s=s, I=tuple(I), vt=vt, g=tuple(g), comp=comp)              # (8) read out g
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), rec
    def init(self, w=None):
        return ([[self.b[i][j] if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(self.n_out)] for i in range(self.n_in)],
                [F(0)] * self.n_out, [F(0)] * self.n_in, [F(0)] * self.n_out)
    def run(self, hist, o=None, **kw):
        o = o or self.init()
        traj, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig, **kw)
            traj.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return traj, recs

def snn_params(S):
    """Reads the K_g parameters from the current global constants of the snn_core module (correspondence between Definition 1 and lines 15–20 of snn_core; main text, Proposition 1)."""
    n_in, n_out = S.N_IN, S.N_OUT
    full = lambda val: [[val] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    return dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=S.PAT, lam=[S.LEAK] * n_out, theta=[S.THETA] * n_out,
                vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[S.DEC] * n_in, dy=[S.DEC] * n_out, Ap=S.A_PLUS, Am=S.A_MINUS,
                b=full(S.W0), lo=full(F(0)), hi=full(S.W_MAX))

def load_snn_core():
    p = SNN_DIR + '/snn_core.py'
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    assert h == FROZEN_SHA['snn_core.py'], 'snn_core.py is not the frozen version'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('snn_core', p)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m, h

def snn_gate(S, rho=F(0)):
    return Gate(rho=rho, **snn_params(S))

def WIN0():
    return [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('B', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 3

def compare_run(S, G, hist, w_init=None, o_init=None, plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """Runs the same history through snn_core.step and through G.step and compares the full state and the plasticity components beat by beat.
    Returns (number of beats, number of clipping events, number of times the gate argument equals the threshold exactly, list of mismatches)."""
    if o_init is not None:
        st = dict(w=[r[:] for r in o_init[0]], v=list(o_init[1]), x=list(o_init[2]), y=list(o_init[3]))
        o = (o_init[0], o_init[1], o_init[2], o_init[3])
    else:
        st = S.init(w_init); o = G.init(w_init)
    bad, nclip, ntie = [], 0, 0
    for t, (inp, teach) in enumerate(hist):
        st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach, plastic=plastic, bap=bap, relax=relax)
        o, r = G.step(o, (inp, teach), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        ntie += sum(1 for j in range(G.n_out) if r['vt'][j] == G.theta[j])
        if st['w'] != o[0] or st['v'] != o[1] or st['x'] != o[2] or st['y'] != o[3]:
            bad.append((t, 'state'))
        if tuple(ev['s_out']) != r['g'] or tuple(ev['s_in']) != tuple(r['s']):
            bad.append((t, 'spikes'))
        for k, (pp, pm, pr, Ssum, nw) in r['comp'].items():
            c = ev['comp'][k]
            w_before = Ssum - pp - pm - pr
            if (c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]) != (pp, pm, pr, nw - w_before):
                bad.append((t, 'comp', k))
            if nw != Ssum:
                nclip += 1
    return len(hist), nclip, ntie, bad

def main():
    S, h = load_snn_core()
    print('1. Frozen-version verification')
    print('  snn_core.py SHA-256 =', h, '(matches frozen value)')
    base = {k: getattr(S, k) for k in ['LEAK', 'THETA', 'DEC', 'A_PLUS', 'A_MINUS', 'W0', 'W_MAX']}
    def setp(**kw):
        for k, val in base.items():
            setattr(S, k, kw.get(k, val))
    G = snn_gate(S)
    print('  Definition 1 parameter domain: λ =', G.lam[0], ', d =', G.dx[0], ', ρ = 0 (RELAX default), A₊ =', G.Ap, ', A₋ =', G.Am,
          ', b =', G.b[0][0], ', [lo, hi] = [', G.lo[0][0], ',', G.hi[0][0], '], θ =', G.theta[0], ', v_reset =',
          G.vreset[0], '; entry vectors', sorted(G.pat.items()), '— all constraints satisfied:', G.check_domain())
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    CTRL = [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
    WIN = WIN0()
    tot = dict(ticks=0, clip=0, tie=0, bad=[])
    def acc(res, label):
        n, c, ti, bad = res
        tot['ticks'] += n; tot['clip'] += c; tot['tie'] += ti; tot['bad'] += [(label,) + b for b in bad]
        return n, c, ti, len(bad)
    print('2. Frozen declared history (as used by snn_instance.py), compared beat by beat: step of K_g Definition 2 vs snn_core.step')
    rows = []
    rows.append(('FORM (formation phase)', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM), 'FORM')))
    rows.append(('CTRL (no-teaching control)', acc(compare_run(S, G, CTRL), 'CTRL')))
    rows.append(('FORM+REST+WIN (with Trace-Response)', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM + REST + WIN), 'FRW')))
    st_r = S.run(REST, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o_er = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('erasure-side WIN (R1(a′): weights reset, v, x, y carried over from the end-of-rest state, plasticity on)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_er), 'ERASE')))
    o_r = (st_r['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('route-cut WIN (bap off)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, bap=False), 'BAPOFF')))
    rows.append(('frozen-plasticity WIN (plastic off)', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, plastic=False), 'FROZEN')))
    rows.append(('relaxation RELAX = 1/10, FORM+REST+WIN', acc(compare_run(S, snn_gate(S, F(1, 10)), FORM + REST + WIN, relax=F(1, 10)), 'RELAX')))
    for lab, (n, c, ti, nb) in rows:
        print(f'  {lab}: {n} beats, clipping {c}, gate argument = θ {ti} times, mismatches {nb}')
    print('3. Parameter grid (the 108 groups of snn_scan.py; snn_core global constants rewritten as it does, K_g parameters read out alongside)')
    ng, tie_grid = 0, 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        setp(W0=W0, A_PLUS=AP)
        Gp = snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        ctrl = ([('A', (0, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, form + REST + WIN0()), 'GRID')[2]
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, ctrl), 'GRIDC')[2]
        ng += 1
    setp()
    print(f'  {ng} groups × 2 histories done; beats with gate argument exactly θ: {tie_grid} (at W0 = 1/2 the baseline input is 1/2 + 1/2 = θ)')
    print('4. Random histories (seed 20261101; 300 histories of length 60; letters cover the four entry patterns and two teaching currents in {0, 1/2, 1, 2};')
    print('    initial weights random rationals in [0, 2]; protocol switches and RELAX ∈ {0, 1/10, 1/2} random)')
    rng = random.Random(20261101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    for _ in range(300):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)])
        Gr = snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(60)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(S.N_OUT)] for _ in range(S.N_IN)]
        acc(compare_run(S, Gr, hist, w_init=w0, plastic=rng.random() < 0.85, bap=rng.random() < 0.85, relax=rho), 'RAND')
    print('5. Saturation history (tests the clipping branch): A_PLUS = 1/2, A_MINUS = 1/2, strong teaching pairings alternating with presynaptic-only firing')
    setp(A_PLUS=F(1, 2), A_MINUS=F(1, 2))
    Gs = snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    n, c, ti, nb = acc(compare_run(S, Gs, sat), 'SAT')
    setp()
    print(f'  {n} beats, clipping {c}, mismatches {nb}')
    print('6. Totals')
    print(f'  beats compared: {tot["ticks"]}; clipping took effect at {tot["clip"]} places; gate argument exactly at threshold {tot["tie"]} times; mismatches at {len(tot["bad"])} places')
    print('  Conclusion (finite verification): on all histories listed above, the per-beat execution of K_g Definition 2 agrees exactly with snn_core.step, beat by beat and component by component, '
          'including the clipping branch and the at-threshold branch.' if not tot['bad'] else '  Mismatches found: ' + repr(tot['bad'][:10]))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.3  deps12.py  Loads A.1 and A.2 and checks their SHA-256

~~~python
"""Loads the frozen dependencies (in the same directory as this program) and checks their SHA-256 before loading.
  snn_core.py   program of the frozen SNN instance s1 (Appendix A.1)
  gate_class.py appendix program of The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts v1.0 (𝒦_g and the declared R-layer instances, Appendix A.2)
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, importlib.util
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
DEP = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SHA = {'snn_core.py': '4e0fd45d2442890ecf3a7687ea285f89ed161e6fb4ab1083ca0fb79821960e49',
       'gate_class.py': 'b07c0327be0383f020de11e03c656e6967df12b972a4e7e97dd0af99885eb630'}

def _load(fn, name):
    p = os.path.join(DEP, fn)
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    assert h == SHA[fn], fn + ' is not the frozen version'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, p)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = m
    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m, h

S, H_CORE = _load('snn_core.py', 'snn_core')
GC, H_GATE = _load('gate_class.py', 'gate_class')
~~~

A.4  common12.py  Common definitions: the validity conditions for m and the m\_α family, the three reading types, Lead\_q, and the values of B and B^q (3.1, 5.2)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Sedimentation-Dynamics Concretization: common definitions (standard library only; exact rational arithmetic).
This module implements the host-independent parts:
  validity conditions for the common-level function m (The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.2, Appendix 1, Section 5, fragment B5: declared in advance, idempotent m(c,…,c) = c, intermediate, symmetric)
  the m_α family: m_α(δ_K) = min + α(max − min), α ∈ [0, 1] ∩ ℚ (Lemma M1: the range of c* over valid m is exactly [min, max] ∩ ℚ)
  three kinds of relative reading: first-opened set Y_first, order of precedence Y_order, share Y_share (fragment B4: "the reading may be the first-opened set, the order of precedence or shares")
  member precedence Lead_q (sole lead / tied lead / not leading; original names in The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.2, Appendix 1, Section 5)
  evaluation of B_m and B_m^q (fragment B5); an undefined reading is recorded as 'undef-reading' and an undefined I_m as 'undef-Im'; neither is judged false
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import itertools
INF = None   # a time with no opening is recorded as None (i.e. +∞)

# ---------- common-level function m ----------
def m_min(ds):
    return min(ds)

def m_max(ds):
    return max(ds)

def m_mean(ds):
    return sum(ds, F(0)) / len(ds)

def m_alpha(alpha):
    alpha = F(alpha)
    assert 0 <= alpha <= 1
    def m(ds):
        return min(ds) + alpha * (max(ds) - min(ds))
    m.__name__ = f'm_alpha({alpha})'
    return m

M_STD = {'min': m_min, 'mean': m_mean, 'max': m_max}

def check_m_legal(m, samples):
    """The three machine-checkable validity conditions out of four: idempotent, intermediate, symmetric (declaration in advance is guaranteed by the procedure). samples is a list of Sedimentation-Degree tuples."""
    for ds in samples:
        ds = [F(d) for d in ds]
        c = ds[0]
        if m([c] * len(ds)) != c:
            return False, ('idempotent', ds)
        v = m(ds)
        if not (min(ds) <= v <= max(ds)):
            return False, ('intermediate', ds)
        for p in itertools.permutations(ds):
            if m(list(p)) != v:
                return False, ('symmetric', ds)
    return True, None

# ---------- readings ----------
def y_first(times):
    """times: {path: first opening beat or None}. Returns the first-opened set (the empty set if nothing opens within W)."""
    fin = [t for t in times.values() if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset()
    t0 = min(fin)
    return frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == t0)

def y_order(times):
    """Order of precedence: the paths that open within W in the weak order of their first opening beats (ties grouped); paths that never open are listed separately. Reads only the relative order, not absolute beats."""
    fin = sorted(set(t for t in times.values() if t is not None))
    groups = tuple(frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == tt) for tt in fin)
    never = frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t is None)
    return (groups, never)

def y_share(counts):
    """Share: counts {path: throughput}; the reading is undefined when the total is 0 (as in the domain condition of FlowShare in Field Chapter 11; fragment C2)."""
    tot = sum(counts.values(), F(0))
    if tot == 0:
        return 'undef'
    return tuple(sorted((p, F(c) / tot) for p, c in counts.items()))

def lead(reading_kind, Y, q):
    """Member precedence Lead_q ∈ {sole lead, tied lead, not leading}; returns 'undef' when the share reading is undefined."""
    if reading_kind == 'first':
        if Y == frozenset([q]):
            return 'sole lead'
        return 'tied lead' if q in Y else 'not leading'
    if reading_kind == 'order':
        groups, never = Y
        if not groups or q not in groups[0]:
            return 'not leading'
        return 'sole lead' if len(groups[0]) == 1 else 'tied lead'
    if reading_kind == 'share':
        if Y == 'undef':
            return 'undef'
        d = dict(Y)
        mx = max(d.values())
        tops = [p for p, v in d.items() if v == mx]
        if q not in tops:
            return 'not leading'
        return 'sole lead' if len(tops) == 1 else 'tied lead'
    raise ValueError(reading_kind)

def B_value(Y0, Y1):
    """Value of B_m: called when I_m is defined. If the reading on either side is undefined, records 'undef-reading' (not judged false)."""
    if Y0 == 'undef' or Y1 == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return Y0 != Y1

def Bq_value(kind, Y0, Y1, q):
    a, b = lead(kind, Y0, q), lead(kind, Y1, q)
    if a == 'undef' or b == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return a != b

def fmt(x):
    if isinstance(x, F):
        return str(x)
    if isinstance(x, frozenset):
        return '{' + ','.join(str(e) for e in sorted(x, key=str)) + '}'
    if isinstance(x, tuple):
        return '(' + ', '.join(fmt(e) for e in x) + ')'
    return str(x)
~~~

A.5  thr\_model.py  Host D: Theorems TH-1, TH-2 and TH-3, and the validity check of Lemma M1 (10.7, 10.8)

~~~python
"""Host D: minimal two-path threshold model (the blocked example and its contrast in Form Chapter 20 v1.2, Appendix 1, Section 5; fragment B6).
Run: python3 thr_model.py
Model (path-level model (A)): two paths a, b under entry e; Depth_x is the state; θ_x = g(Depth_x); the entry flow pressure p(t) follows a declared (nondecreasing) sequence;
the blockage Brk is a frozen quantity; τ_x = min{t ∈ W : Brk_x = 0 and p(t) ≥ θ_x} (None if there is none).
Two run conventions: 'stop' (stop as soon as some path opens); 'keep' (a path stays open once opened and the run does not stop; a declared augmentation M⁺ set up in this paper for the share reading).
Share reading (only under 'keep'): the ratio of the numbers of beats in which each path is open within W.
I_m: set the Depth of both members of K = {a, b} to c* = m(Depth_K) and recompute θ by g; Brk and p are frozen (fragment B5).
This program checks finite cases of Theorems TH-1, TH-2, TH-3 and Lemma M1 of the main text (proofs in Section 10 of the main text).
"""
import sys, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import (y_first, y_order, y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha,
                      check_m_legal, fmt)
PATHS = ('a', 'b')

def g_common(d):
    return 1 / (1 + F(d))           # same threshold mechanism, strictly decreasing in the Sedimentation-Degree

def run(depth, p, brk=frozenset(), g=None, mode='stop'):
    g = g or {x: g_common for x in PATHS}
    th = {x: g[x](depth[x]) for x in PATHS}
    tau = {}
    for x in PATHS:
        tau[x] = None
        if x in brk:
            continue
        for t, pt in enumerate(p):
            if pt >= th[x]:
                tau[x] = t
                break
    if mode == 'stop':
        fin = [t for t in tau.values() if t is not None]
        if fin:
            t0 = min(fin)
            tau = {x: (t if (t is not None and t == t0) else None) for x, t in tau.items()}
    counts = {x: (len(p) - tau[x] if tau[x] is not None else 0) for x in PATHS}
    return tau, counts

def readings(depth, p, brk, g, mode):
    tau, counts = run(depth, p, brk, g, mode)
    Y = {'first': y_first(tau), 'order': y_order(tau)}
    if mode == 'keep':
        Y['share'] = y_share(counts)
    return Y

def I_m(depth, m):
    c = m([depth[x] for x in PATHS])
    return {x: c for x in PATHS}, c

MFAM = [m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4)), m_alpha(F(3, 4)), m_alpha(F(1, 3))]
DEPTHS = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2), F(3)]
PVALS = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(2, 5), F(3, 5), F(4, 5), F(1)]

def psequences(n=4):
    for comb in itertools.combinations_with_replacement(range(len(PVALS)), n):
        yield [PVALS[i] for i in comb]

def main():
    print('1. Validity of m (idempotent, intermediate, symmetric)')
    samples = [(0, 1), (1, 0), (2, 2), (0, 3, 1), (F(1, 2), F(7, 3))]
    for m in MFAM:
        print('  ', m.__name__, check_m_legal(m, samples))
    print('2. Theorem TH-1 (both paths unobstructed, the same strictly decreasing threshold mechanism, p nondecreasing): under the first-opened reading, B_m ⇔ Y is a singleton ⇔ D ∧ P17 ∧ the deeper path opens within W; independent of m')
    n = viol = 0
    nB = 0
    for da, db in itertools.product(DEPTHS, DEPTHS):
        for p in psequences():
            for mode in ('stop', 'keep'):
                depth = {'a': da, 'b': db}
                Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset(), None, mode)
                vals = set()
                for m in MFAM:
                    d1, c = I_m(depth, m)
                    Y1 = readings(d1, p, frozenset(), None, mode)
                    vals.add(B_value(Y0['first'], Y1['first']))
                n += 1
                singleton = len(Y0['first']) == 1
                # D ∧ P17 ∧ the deeper path opens within W
                if da != db:
                    deep = 'a' if da > db else 'b'
                    sh = 'b' if deep == 'a' else 'a'
                    tau, _ = run(depth, p, frozenset(), None, 'keep')
                    cond = tau[deep] is not None and p[tau[deep]] < g_common(depth[sh])
                else:
                    cond = False
                if len(vals) != 1 or vals.pop() != singleton or singleton != cond:
                    viol += 1
                nB += singleton
    print(f'  cases {n} (depth pairs 25 × flow-pressure sequences 126 × two run conventions), 6 valid m per case; violations {viol}; cases with B true {nB}')
    print('3. Theorem TH-2 (a completely blocked): B_m^first is true ⇔ [p reaches g(Depth_b) within W] ≠ [p reaches g(c*) within W];')
    print('    hence "false for every valid m" ⇔ p reaches g(min Depth_K) within W ∨ p does not reach g(max Depth_K) within W; in between, B_m varies with m')
    n = viol = vary = 0
    for da, db in itertools.product(DEPTHS, DEPTHS):
        for p in psequences():
            depth = {'a': da, 'b': db}
            Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
            tau0, _ = run(depth, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
            Bs = []
            for m in MFAM:
                d1, c = I_m(depth, m)
                Y1 = readings(d1, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
                B = B_value(Y0['first'], Y1['first'])
                pred = (max(p) >= g_common(db)) != (max(p) >= g_common(c))
                viol += (B != pred)
                Bs.append(B)
            allfalse_pred = max(p) >= g_common(min(da, db)) or max(p) < g_common(max(da, db))
            viol += ((not any(Bs)) != allfalse_pred)
            vary += (len(set(Bs)) > 1)
            n += 1
    print(f'  cases {n}; violations {viol}; cases in which B_m varies with m under the same threshold mechanism {vary}')
    depth = {'a': F(0), 'b': F(3)}
    p = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(2, 5), F(2, 5)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
    print('  Witness A (same threshold mechanism, shallower a blocked, deeper b unobstructed, p between g(max) and g(min)): Depth = (0, 3), p =', fmt(tuple(p)), ', original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']))
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        d1, c = I_m(depth, m)
        Y1 = readings(d1, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
        print('    ', m.__name__, 'c* =', c, 'θ* =', g_common(c), 'Y′ =', fmt(Y1['first']), 'B =', B_value(Y0['first'], Y1['first']))
    depth = {'a': F(3), 'b': F(1)}
    p = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(2, 5), F(2, 5)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
    print('  Witness B (deeper a blocked, shallower b unobstructed and originally not opening, p between g(max) and g(min)): Depth = (3, 1), p =', fmt(tuple(p)), ', original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']))
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        d1, c = I_m(depth, m)
        Y1 = readings(d1, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
        print('    ', m.__name__, 'c* =', c, 'θ* =', g_common(c), 'Y′ =', fmt(Y1['first']), 'B =', B_value(Y0['first'], Y1['first']))
    depth = {'a': F(3), 'b': F(0)}
    p = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(2, 5), F(1)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')
    print('  Contrast (blocked-example type: deeper a blocked, p reaches g(min)): original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']), '; B(min/mean/max) =',
          [B_value(Y0['first'], readings(I_m(depth, m)[0], p, frozenset('a'), None, 'stop')['first']) for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max)])
    print('4. Theorem TH-3 (run convention keep): when the readings are defined on both sides, B_m has the same truth value under all three kinds of reading (first-opened set, order of precedence, share);')
    print('    the share reading is undefined ⇔ no path opens within W before or after the intervention')
    n = viol = und = 0
    for da, db in itertools.product(DEPTHS, DEPTHS):
        for p in psequences():
            for brk in (frozenset(), frozenset('a'), frozenset('b')):
                depth = {'a': da, 'b': db}
                Y0 = readings(depth, p, brk, None, 'keep')
                for m in MFAM:
                    d1, c = I_m(depth, m)
                    Y1 = readings(d1, p, brk, None, 'keep')
                    bs = {k: B_value(Y0[k], Y1[k]) for k in ('first', 'order', 'share')}
                    n += 1
                    if bs['share'] == 'undef-reading':
                        und += 1
                        no_open = (Y0['first'] == frozenset()) or (Y1['first'] == frozenset())
                        viol += (not no_open)
                        continue
                    viol += not (bs['first'] == bs['order'] == bs['share'])
    print(f'  values {n}; violations {viol}; share reading undefined {und}')
    print('5. A resolution-level source of D holding while B_m fails (P17 fails: one step crosses both thresholds)')
    depth = {'a': F(2), 'b': F(1)}
    p = [F(0), F(1)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')
    print('  Depth = (2, 1), θ = (1/3, 1/2), p = (0, 1): original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']), '; B(min/mean/max) =',
          [B_value(Y0['first'], readings(I_m(depth, m)[0], p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')['first']) for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max)])
    p = [F(0), F(2, 5), F(1)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')
    print('  same depths, p = (0, 2/5, 1): original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']), '; B(min/mean/max) =',
          [B_value(Y0['first'], readings(I_m(depth, m)[0], p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')['first']) for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max)])
    print('6. With heterogeneous threshold mechanisms, B_m varies with m (heterogeneous-threshold example: θ_a = 1/(1+d), θ_b = 2/(1+d)²)')
    g = {'a': lambda d: 1 / (1 + F(d)), 'b': lambda d: 2 / (1 + F(d)) ** 2}
    depth = {'a': F(0), 'b': F(3)}
    p = [F(k, 20) for k in range(0, 41)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset(), g, 'stop')
    print('  Depth = (0, 3): original Y =', fmt(Y0['first']), '; B(min/mean/max) =',
          [B_value(Y0['first'], readings(I_m(depth, m)[0], p, frozenset(), g, 'stop')['first']) for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max)])
    print('7. B_m^q and B_m: one direction only (a TH-1 case, with q the shallower path):')
    depth = {'a': F(2), 'b': F(0)}
    p = [F(0), F(2, 5), F(2, 5)]
    Y0 = readings(depth, p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')
    d1, c = I_m(depth, m_min)
    Y1 = readings(d1, p, frozenset(), None, 'stop')
    print('  Depth = (2, 0), p = (0, 2/5, 2/5), m = min: Y =', fmt(Y0['first']), '→', fmt(Y1['first']),
          '; B =', B_value(Y0['first'], Y1['first']), '; B^b =', Bq_value('first', Y0['first'], Y1['first'], 'b'),
          '; B^a =', Bq_value('first', Y0['first'], Y1['first'], 'a'))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.6  fam\_jia\_wu.py  Hosts B and C: I\_m, Theorems W-2, J-1 and W-1, sources of D∧¬B, and the F/Ω/Bias case table (10.4, 10.11, 11.3, 11.4)

~~~python
"""Hosts B and C: I_m, B_m (share reading), F, Ω_W and Bias on Family A (constant-head two-channel flow network) and Family E (firing-rate neural network) of the Construction Form.
Run: python3 fam_jia_wu.py
The physical mechanism models are rewritten term by term from the constitutive antecedents and parameters of The Construction Form of Field Concretization v1.0, 4.2 and 4.3 (Sections 4.2 and 4.3 of that paper), in exact rational arithmetic:
  Family A: Qᵢ = (g₀ + κ_cons zᵢ)ΔP; write φcQᵢΔt/ℓ; relaxation −γzᵢΔt goes into the store Lᵢ; store output min(Lᵢ, eQᵢΔt) (store at the start of the beat); seed dumps 2 A_u into channel 1.
  Family E: yᵢ = β(w₀ + zᵢ)x; write ηxyᵢΔt; decay −γzᵢΔt; receptor pool Rᵢ −= (write + induction − decay)/g₁; pair induces +2 S_u at synapse 1.
Notation: the conductance coefficient κ of C1 in The Construction Form of Field Concretization and the continuation tendency κ of Field Chapter 1, Definition 11 share a name but not a meaning; this paper writes the former as κ_cons.
Declared augmentation (marked "aug", not part of the original declaration of The Construction Form of Field Concretization): Family A⁺ adds a frozen blockage flag brkᵢ (when brkᵢ = 1, gᵢ = 0 and the channel does not conduct).
Reading: share Y_share = the ratio of ΣQᵢ (Family A) or Σyᵢ (Family E) within W; neither family has an outflow threshold, so the first-opened and order-of-precedence readings have no object (Field Chapter 1, Equation 4 and Theorem 17 have no direct object).
I_m (path-level model (A), path = channel / synapse, Depth = zᵢ): z₁ = z₂ = c*; Lᵢ, brk and the input protocol are frozen; the receptor pool of Family E follows, recomputed by N5 conservation (write-back rule R-pool, see 10.4 of the main text).
"""
import sys, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha, fmt
# ---------------- Family A ----------------
JIA = dict(g0=F(1), kap=F(1), c=F(1, 5), phi=F(1, 2), ell=F(1), gam=F(1, 10), e=F(1, 5), zmax=F(10))

def jia_step(st, letter, P=JIA, brk=(0, 0)):
    z, L = list(st['z']), list(st['L'])
    dP = F(1) if letter == 'flow' else F(0)
    Q = [(0 if brk[i] else (P['g0'] + P['kap'] * z[i])) * dP for i in range(2)]
    exp_ = [min(L[i], P['e'] * Q[i]) for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['phi'] * P['c'] * Q[i] / P['ell'] for i in range(2)]
    rel = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - rel[i] for i in range(2)]
    L2 = [L[i] + rel[i] * P['ell'] - exp_[i] for i in range(2)]
    if letter == 'seed':
        z2[0] += 2
    assert all(0 <= v <= P['zmax'] for v in z2) and all(v >= 0 for v in L2), 'outside the domain of validity'
    return dict(z=z2, L=L2), Q

def jia_run(st, hist, P=JIA, brk_sched=None):
    Qs = []
    for t, a in enumerate(hist):
        brk = brk_sched[t] if brk_sched else (0, 0)
        st, Q = jia_step(st, a, P, brk)
        Qs.append(Q)
    return st, Qs

# ---------------- Family E ----------------
WU = dict(w0=F(1), beta=F(1), eta=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), g1=F(1, 10), ymax=F(100))

def wu_step(st, letter, P=WU):
    z, R = list(st['z']), list(st['R'])
    x = F(1) if letter == 'drive' else F(0)
    y = [P['beta'] * (P['w0'] + z[i]) * x for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['eta'] * x * y[i] for i in range(2)]
    dec = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    ind = [F(2) if (letter == 'pair' and i == 0) else F(0) for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - dec[i] + ind[i] for i in range(2)]
    R2 = [R[i] - (wr[i] + ind[i] - dec[i]) / P['g1'] for i in range(2)]
    phys = all(v >= 0 for v in R2) and all(v <= P['ymax'] for v in y) and all(v >= 0 for v in z2)
    return dict(z=z2, R=R2), y, phys

def wu_run(st, hist, P=WU):
    ys, ok = [], True
    for a in hist:
        st, y, phys = wu_step(st, a, P)
        ys.append(y); ok = ok and phys
    return st, ys, ok

# ---------------- readings and I_m ----------------
def share_of(series, beats=None):
    beats = range(len(series)) if beats is None else beats
    return y_share({i + 1: sum((series[t][i] for t in beats), F(0)) for i in range(2)})

def Im_jia(st, m):
    c = m(st['z'])
    ext = sum((c - zi) * JIA['ell'] for zi in st['z'])       # volume of structural material exchanged with outside B_phys (positive = added)
    return dict(z=[c, c], L=list(st['L'])), c, ext

def Im_wu(st, m, rule='pool'):
    """rule='pool': the receptor pool follows by N5 conservation (R is a quantity determined by Sedimentation-Degree); rule='frozen': the pool is frozen (recorded as physically invalid when N5 is violated)."""
    c = m(st['z'])
    if rule == 'pool':
        R = [st['R'][i] - (c - st['z'][i]) / WU['g1'] for i in range(2)]
        feasible = all(r >= 0 for r in R)
    else:
        R = list(st['R'])
        feasible = all(c == zi for zi in st['z'])               # a frozen pool with changed conductance violates receptor conservation N5
    return dict(z=[c, c], R=R), c, feasible

MFAM = [m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4)), m_alpha(F(3, 4))]

def thmJ1_check():
    """Finite check of Theorems J-1 / W-1: Family A (κ_cons ≥ 0, 0 ≤ γ ≤ 1, W contains only flow, rest) and Family E (W contains only drive, rest):
       the share reading is defined ⇔ W contains a conducting beat; when defined, B_m ⇔ κ_eff > 0 ∧ ∃ conducting beat t with d(t) ≠ 0; independent of m; after I_m, z₁ ≡ z₂."""
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n = viol = 0
    zs = [F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2), F(5)]
    for kap in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for gam in (F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1)):
            P = dict(JIA, kap=kap, gam=gam)
            for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
                for _ in range(6):
                    L = rng.randint(1, 6)
                    hist = [rng.choice(['flow', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
                    st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P)
                    Y0 = share_of(Q0)
                    # prediction
                    d = z1 - z2
                    pred_true = False
                    for a in hist:
                        if a == 'flow':
                            if kap > 0 and d != 0:
                                pred_true = True
                            d = d * (1 + P['phi'] * P['c'] * kap - gam)
                        else:
                            d = d * (1 - gam)
                    defined = 'flow' in hist
                    for m in MFAM:
                        c = m([z1, z2])
                        st1 = dict(z=[c, c], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                        st_end, Q1 = jia_run(st1, hist, P)
                        Y1 = share_of(Q1)
                        B = B_value(Y0, Y1)
                        n += 1
                        if not defined:
                            viol += (B != 'undef-reading')
                        else:
                            viol += (B != pred_true)
                        viol += (st_end['z'][0] != st_end['z'][1])
    nw = vw = 0
    for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
        for _ in range(6):
            L = rng.randint(1, 6)
            hist = [rng.choice(['drive', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
            st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], R=[F(100), F(100)])
            _, y0, _ = wu_run(st0, hist)
            Y0 = share_of(y0)
            d = z1 - z2
            pred = False
            for a in hist:
                if a == 'drive':
                    pred = pred or d != 0
                    d = d * (1 + WU['eta'] * WU['beta'] - WU['gam'])
                else:
                    d = d * (1 - WU['gam'])
            for m in MFAM:
                st1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
                _, y1, _ = wu_run(st1, hist)
                B = B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))
                nw += 1
                vw += (B != ('undef-reading' if 'drive' not in hist else pred))
    return n, viol, nw, vw

def subsegments(n_beats, k):
    size = n_beats // k
    return [list(range(i * size, (i + 1) * size)) for i in range(k)]

def relations_jia(label, st0, hist, brk_sched=None, P=JIA, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), m=m_mean, q=0):
    """Computes D, B_m (share), B_m^q, F(q), Ω_W(q) and Bias(q against the other path) on Family A (or Family A⁺).
    Declared: k equal sub-segments; Pass = number of conducting beats (Qᵢ > 0); the habitual-travel criterion for Familiar Routes and the re-traversal criterion for Ω both take Pass ≥ k; the third item of Ω is that every sub-segment has a conducting beat;
    Brk_W(q) = 1 if and only if brk_q = 1 at some beat within W; Bias: (4a) z_q − z_o > ε_δ at the start of W, (4b) the flow share of q > 1/2 in every sub-segment (operationalization of stable biased continuation over a long window)."""
    o = 1 - q
    segs = subsegments(len(hist), k)
    st, Qs = jia_run(dict(st0), hist, P, brk_sched)
    Y0 = share_of(Qs)
    stI, c, ext = Im_jia(st0, m)
    _, QsI = jia_run(stI, hist, P, brk_sched)
    B = B_value(Y0, share_of(QsI))
    D = st0['z'][0] != st0['z'][1]
    passes = sum(1 for Q in Qs if Q[q] > 0)
    fam = st0['z'][q] > 0 and passes >= k
    # F: comparison task T_k per sub-segment, with the start state taken from the actual run
    Fok = fam
    cur = dict(st0)
    seg_lead = []
    for sg in segs:
        sub = [hist[t] for t in sg]
        sub_brk = [brk_sched[t] for t in sg] if brk_sched else None
        _, Qk = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
        Yk = share_of(Qk)
        cI, _, _ = Im_jia(cur, m)
        _, QkI = jia_run(cI, sub, P, sub_brk)
        YkI = share_of(QkI)
        ld = lead('share', Yk, q + 1)
        bq = Bq_value('share', Yk, YkI, q + 1)
        seg_lead.append((ld, bq))
        Fok = Fok and ld == 'sole lead' and bq is True
        cur, _ = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
    brkq = any(b[q] for b in brk_sched) if brk_sched else False
~~~
