A.7  agg12.py  Theorem AGG: projection and tangent-cone criteria for linear aggregation, and the position-wise bottleneck criterion (10.2)

~~~python
"""Definedness and uniqueness of the I_m write-back under aggregation model (B) (Theorem AGG of the main text).
Run: python3 agg12.py
Linear aggregation: write-back set S = {z′ ∈ ℚ^{X_K} : A z′ = c*·1, ℓ ≤ z′ ≤ h}.
  Criterion (1): S is a single point ⇔ the projection of S onto every coordinate is a single point (exact Fourier–Motzkin projection).
  Criterion (2): let z₀ ∈ S. S = {z₀} ⇔ ker A ∩ T(z₀) = {0}, where T(z₀) is the tangent cone of the box at z₀ (dₓ ≥ 0 where z₀ₓ = ℓ, dₓ ≤ 0 where z₀ₓ = h).
  Corollary: when c* lies strictly inside (ℓ, h) and z₀ = c*·1 is feasible (mean type), T is the whole space and uniqueness ⇔ rank A = |X_K|.
Bottleneck aggregation (Depth = minimum along the path): S = {z′ ∈ [c*, h]^{X_K} : every path has at least one position = c*};
  S is a single point ⇔ c* = h, or there is no position "all of whose paths have length ≥ 2" (i.e. every position alone makes up some single-position path).
The program also rechecks the bottleneck criterion by exhaustive subset search. Exact rational arithmetic; all parameters are taken in ℚ.
"""
import sys, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
Z = F(0)

def rank(M):
    M = [list(r) for r in M]
    r = 0
    for c in range(len(M[0]) if M else 0):
        piv = next((i for i in range(r, len(M)) if M[i][c] != 0), None)
        if piv is None:
            continue
        M[r], M[piv] = M[piv], M[r]
        for i in range(len(M)):
            if i != r and M[i][c] != 0:
                f = M[i][c] / M[r][c]
                M[i] = [a - f * b for a, b in zip(M[i], M[r])]
        r += 1
    return r

def fm_project(cons, n, k):
    """cons: [(a, b)] means a·z ≤ b. Eliminates every variable other than k and returns (feasible?, lower bound, upper bound) (None means unbounded)."""
    cur = [(list(a), b) for a, b in cons]
    for j in range(n):
        if j == k:
            continue
        P = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] > 0]
        N = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] < 0]
        new = [c for c in cur if c[0][j] == 0]
        for ap, bp in P:
            for an, bn in N:
                fp, fn = ap[j], -an[j]
                a = [fn * x + fp * y for x, y in zip(ap, an)]
                b = fn * bp + fp * bn
                new.append((a, b))
        # normalize and deduplicate
        seen, cur = set(), []
        for a, b in new:
            s = next((abs(x) for x in a if x != 0), None)
            if s is None:
                if b < 0:
                    return (False, None, None)
                continue
            key = (tuple(x / s for x in a), b / s)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                cur.append((list(key[0]), key[1]))
    lo = hi = None
    for a, b in cur:
        if a[k] > 0:
            v = b / a[k]
            hi = v if hi is None else min(hi, v)
        elif a[k] < 0:
            v = b / a[k]
            lo = v if lo is None else max(lo, v)
    if lo is not None and hi is not None and lo > hi:
        return (False, lo, hi)
    return (True, lo, hi)

def poly(A, rhs, lo, hi):
    n = len(lo)
    cons = []
    for row, r in zip(A, rhs):
        cons.append((list(row), r))
        cons.append(([-x for x in row], -r))
    for i in range(n):
        e = [Z] * n; e[i] = F(1)
        cons.append((e, hi[i]))
        cons.append(([-x for x in e], -lo[i]))
    return cons

def linear_writeback(A, c, lo, hi, z0=None):
    n = len(lo)
    cons = poly(A, [c] * len(A), lo, hi)
    proj = []
    feas = True
    for k in range(n):
        ok, a, b = fm_project(cons, n, k)
        feas = feas and ok
        proj.append((a, b))
    unique = feas and all(a == b for a, b in proj)
    res = dict(feasible=feas, unique=unique, proj=[(str(a), str(b)) for a, b in proj])
    # criterion (2): check ker A ∩ tangent cone at a known feasible point z0
    if feas:
        if z0 is None:
            z0 = [a for a, b in proj] if unique else None
            if z0 is None:
                # get a feasible point: fix coordinates one by one at the lower bound of the projection, then project again (exact)
                z0 = []
                cc = list(cons)
                for k in range(n):
                    ok, a, b = fm_project(cc, n, k)
                    v = a if a is not None else (b if b is not None else Z)
                    z0.append(v)
                    e = [Z] * n; e[k] = F(1)
                    cc = cc + [(e, v), ([-x for x in e], -v)]
        dcons = poly(A, [Z] * len(A), [F(-1)] * n, [F(1)] * n)
        for i in range(n):
            e = [Z] * n; e[i] = F(1)
            if z0[i] == lo[i]:
                dcons.append(([-x for x in e], Z))
            if z0[i] == hi[i]:
                dcons.append((e, Z))
        nz = False
        for k in range(n):
            ok, a, b = fm_project(dcons, n, k)
            if ok and ((a is not None and a < 0) or (b is not None and b > 0)):
                nz = True
        res['cone_unique'] = not nz
        res['z0'] = z0
    else:
        res['cone_unique'] = None
    return res

def bottleneck_unique(paths, n, c, h):
    if c == h:
        return True
    for x in range(n):
        if all(len(p) >= 2 for p in paths if x in p):
            return False
    return True

def bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, n, c, h):
    if c == h:
        return True
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        for R in itertools.combinations(range(n), k):
            if all(any(y not in R for y in p) for p in paths):
                return False
    return True

def random_linear_check(N, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    n_ok = viol = nint = vint = 0
    for _ in range(N):
        n = rng.randint(2, 4)
        m = rng.randint(1, 3)
        A = [[F(rng.randint(0, 2), rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n)] for _ in range(m)]
        if any(all(x == 0 for x in r) for r in A):
            continue
        kind = rng.choice(['mean', 'raw'])
        if kind == 'mean':
            A = [[x / sum(r) for x in r] for r in A]
        lo, hi = [Z] * n, [F(1)] * n
        c = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        r = linear_writeback(A, c, lo, hi)
        if not r['feasible']:
            continue
        n_ok += 1
        viol += (r['unique'] != r['cone_unique'])
        if kind == 'mean' and 0 < c < 1:
            nint += 1
            vint += (r['unique'] != (rank(A) == n))
    return n_ok, viol, nint, vint

def random_bottleneck_check(N, seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    viol = 0
    for _ in range(N):
        n = rng.randint(1, 5)
        paths = []
        for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 5)):
            k = rng.randint(1, n)
            paths.append(tuple(sorted(rng.sample(range(n), k))))
        cov = set(x for p in paths for x in p)
        if cov != set(range(n)):
            continue
        h = F(1)
        c = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(1)])
        viol += (bottleneck_unique(paths, n, c, h) != bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, n, c, h))
    return N, viol

def main():
    z0 = F(219345, 524288)
    box = (F(-1, 4), F(7, 4))                     # SNN: z ∈ [lo − b, hi − b]
    print('1. Write-back for each K of s1 at beat 26 (linear aggregation; the box is the state interval [−1/4, 7/4] of z; c* ∈ [min, max] ∩ ℚ)')
    cases = [
        ('bifurcation K, mean', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))], 'mean'),
        ('convergence K, mean', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0))], 'mean'),
        ('single-synapse K, path level', [((0, 0),), ((0, 1),)], 'mean'),
        ('nested K = {path A, input-0 branch, input-1 branch}, sum', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 0),), ((1, 0),)], 'sum'),
        ('bifurcation K, sum', [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))], 'sum'),
    ]
    z = {(0, 0): z0, (1, 0): z0}
    for name, paths, agg in cases:
        pos = sorted(set(x for p in paths for x in p))
        A = [[(F(1, len(p)) if agg == 'mean' else F(1)) if x in p else Z for x in pos] for p in paths]
        ds = [sum(A[k][pos.index(x)] * z.get(x, Z) for x in pos) for k in range(len(paths))]
        out = []
        for lab, c in (('min', min(ds)), ('mean', sum(ds) / len(ds)), ('max', max(ds))):
            r = linear_writeback(A, c, [box[0]] * len(pos), [box[1]] * len(pos))
            out.append(f'{lab}: c*={c} solvable={r["feasible"]} unique={r["unique"]} unique-by-tangent-cone={r["cone_unique"]}')
        print(f'  {name}: positions traversed {len(pos)}, rank A = {rank(A)}; ' + '; '.join(out))
    print('2. Boundary examples and checks of the criteria')
    A = [[F(1), Z, Z], [Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]]
    r = linear_writeback(A, Z, [Z] * 3, [F(1)] * 3)
    print(f'  linear: A = [(1,0,0),(0,1/2,1/2)], c* = 0, box [0,1]³: projections {r["proj"]}, unique {r["unique"]}, tangent-cone criterion {r["cone_unique"]}, rank {rank(A)}')
    for c in (F(1, 2), F(1)):
        print(f'  bottleneck: K = {{x}},{{y}},{{x,y}}, c* = {c}: unique by path-wise criterion {bottleneck_unique([(0,), (1,), (0, 1)], 2, c, F(1))}, exhaustive {bottleneck_unique_brute([(0,), (1,), (0, 1)], 2, c, F(1))}')
    print('  bottleneck: s1 bifurcation K (two paths of two synapses each, no overlap), c* = mean: unique by path-wise criterion', bottleneck_unique([(0, 1), (2, 3)], 4, z0 / 2, box[1]))
    n, v, ni, vi = random_linear_check(2000, 20261102)
    print(f'  linear randomized check, {n} cases: violations of agreement between criteria (1) and (2) {v}; on {ni} interior mean-type cases, violations of agreement with the rank criterion {vi}')
    n, v = random_bottleneck_check(3000, 7)
    print(f'  bottleneck randomized check, {n} cases: violations of agreement between the path-wise criterion and exhaustive search {v}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.8  snn12.py  Host A: I\_m, the valid domain and the parameter machine, Theorems S-PAR, S-S1 and S-FORK with its Corollary 1, S-INV, reading types, F/Ω/Bias, Theorem R-B, degeneration (10.3, 10.6, 10.9, 10.10, 10.13, 11)

~~~python
"""Host A: I_m, B_m, B_m^q, F, Ω_W, Bias and degeneration mechanisms on the frozen SNN instance s1.
Run: python3 snn12.py
The R-layer representation follows Chapter 11, The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts v1.0: 𝒦_g, Definitions 1–3; Proposition 1 (frozen SNN ∈ 𝒦_g); declared R-layer instances, Definitions 4–7.
This program imports the Chapter 11 appendix program gate_class.py (SHA-256 checked before loading) and does no encoding of its own; the frozen snn_core.py is used only to reproduce and check the s1 declared history.
The only additions in this paper: the levelling intervention I_m (each synapse in K gets w := b + c*, the rest of the state is frozen) and its rerun, the three kinds of reading, and exact solution in the parameter c* (parameter machine).
Path readings (main text 3.2):
  bifurcation (fork): entry group A_in = {inputs 0, 1} (pattern A); path j = {w0j, w1j}, j = 0, 1; opening event = s₀ = s₁ = 1 and g_j = 1 within a beat.
  convergence (conv): path A = {w00, w10}, path B = {w20, w30}, both converging on output 0; firings of output 0 are attributed by the entry pattern of the beat (share reading only).
  single synapse (single): path j = {w0j} (path level; shares its entry with the non-K w1j) — a contrast in which the threshold mechanisms are not identical.
Aggregation model (B): Depth(path) = arithmetic mean of z over the synapses of the path.
Write-back rule: the main rule is R-shift — every synapse in path P shifts by the same amount c* − Depth(P) (the identity when depths are equal); I_m is undefined if the state interval is left.
  R-uniform (every synapse in K set to z = c*) is used only when each path is internally uniform (then R-uniform = R-shift) or the antecedents of S-INV hold; Theorem S-FORK is stated with R-uniform.
The parameter c always takes values in ℚ; "c* ∈ [min, max]" always means [min, max] ∩ ℚ.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, importlib.util, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import (y_first, y_order, y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha, fmt)
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from deps12 import S, GC, H_CORE, H_GATE
# s1 declared history (identical, character for character, to lines 7–11 of snn_instance.py)
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
WIN = [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('B', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 3
K_FORK = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
K_CONV = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0))]
K_SINGLE = [((0, 0),), ((0, 1),)]
A_IN = (0, 1)

def s1_state():
    st, logf = S.run(FORM)
    st, _ = S.run(REST, st=st)
    _, logw = S.run(WIN, st=st)
    assert S.log_hash(logf).startswith('707404eb6302adc8') and S.log_hash(logw).startswith('84d320db9a654be6')
    return (tuple(tuple(r) for r in st['w']), tuple(st['v']), tuple(st['x']), tuple(st['y']))

def as_lists(o):
    return ([list(r) for r in o[0]], list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))

def depths(G, o, K):
    w = o[0]
    return [sum((w[i][j] - G.b[i][j] for (i, j) in path), F(0)) / len(path) for path in K]

RULE = 'shift'

def shared_positions(K):
    seen = set()
    for path in K:
        for k in path:
            if k in seen:
                return True
            seen.add(k)
    return False

def legal_domain(G, o, K, rule=None):
    """The set of c for which I_c is defined under the write-back rule, intersected with [min Depth, max Depth] (a closed interval with rational endpoints); None if empty.
    R-shift: ∩_{P, x∈P} [lo_x − w_x + Depth(P), hi_x − w_x + Depth(P)]; R-uniform: ∩_x [lo_x − b_x, hi_x − b_x];
    R-shift with shared Structural Positions: returns 'reject'."""
    rule = rule or RULE
    if rule == 'shift' and shared_positions(K):
        return 'reject'
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    L, U = min(ds), max(ds)
    for path, d in zip(K, ds):
        for (i, j) in path:
            if rule == 'shift':
                L = max(L, G.lo[i][j] - o[0][i][j] + d); U = min(U, G.hi[i][j] - o[0][i][j] + d)
            else:
                L = max(L, G.lo[i][j] - G.b[i][j]); U = min(U, G.hi[i][j] - G.b[i][j])
    return (L, U) if L <= U else None

def I_c(G, o, K, c, rule=None):
    """Levelling intervention. rule='shift' (R-shift): each synapse in the path shifts by c − Depth(P); rule='uniform' (R-uniform): each synapse is set to b + c.
    v, x, y, the protocol, the switches and clipping are frozen. Returns None (I_m undefined) when [lo, hi] is left."""
    rule = rule or RULE
    if rule == 'shift' and shared_positions(K):
        return None                                   # R-shift does not apply to shared Structural Positions: explicit rejection
    w = [list(r) for r in o[0]]
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    for path, d in zip(K, ds):
        for (i, j) in path:
            w[i][j] = (o[0][i][j] + (c - d)) if rule == 'shift' else (G.b[i][j] + c)
            if not (G.lo[i][j] <= w[i][j] <= G.hi[i][j]):
                return None
    return (w, list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))

def run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Returns (final state, record); the record holds (entry vector s, gating g) for each beat."""
    o = as_lists(o)
    rec = []
    for sig in hist:
        o, r = G.step(o, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        rec.append((tuple(r['s']), tuple(r['g'])))
    return o, rec

# ---------- readings ----------
def times_counts(rec, reading):
    if reading == 'fork':
        tau, cnt = {0: None, 1: None}, {0: 0, 1: 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if all(s[i] for i in A_IN):
                for j in (0, 1):
                    if g[j]:
                        cnt[j] += 1
                        if tau[j] is None:
                            tau[j] = t
        return tau, cnt
    if reading == 'single':                     # path j = input 0 → output j; opening = s₀ = 1 and g_j = 1
        tau, cnt = {0: None, 1: None}, {0: 0, 1: 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if s[0]:
                for j in (0, 1):
                    if g[j]:
                        cnt[j] += 1
                        if tau[j] is None:
                            tau[j] = t
        return tau, cnt
    if reading == 'conv':                       # firings of output 0 are attributed by entry pattern; A and B in the same beat (AB) are not attributed
        tau, cnt = {'A': None, 'B': None}, {'A': 0, 'B': 0}
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec):
            if g[0]:
                a, b = s[0] and s[1], s[2] and s[3]
                if a and not b:
                    lab = 'A'
                elif b and not a:
                    lab = 'B'
                else:
                    continue
                cnt[lab] += 1
                if tau[lab] is None:
                    tau[lab] = t
        return tau, cnt
    raise ValueError(reading)

def Ys(rec, reading):
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, reading)
    Y = {'share': y_share(cnt)}
    if reading != 'conv':                       # C₌ fails under the convergence reading, so order-type readings are not set (main text 3.2)
        Y['first'] = y_first(tau)
        Y['order'] = y_order(tau)
    return Y

# ---------- parameter machine: exact piecewise solution for c ∈ (L, U) ----------
def aff_eval(f, c):
    return f[0] + f[1] * c

def param_explore(G, o, K, hist, L, U, plastic=True, bap=True, rule=None):
    """Synapses in K take w = b + c (c a parameter); the rest of the state is numeric. Returns [(kind, lo, hi, rec)]: kind 'open' means the record is constant on the open interval (lo, hi),
    'point' means the isolated point c = lo. Gating comparisons and clipping comparisons both compare an affine function of c with a constant; each beat is split at the roots (Theorem S-PAR of the main text)."""
    rule = rule or RULE
    dom = legal_domain(G, o, K, rule)
    if dom in (None, 'reject') or not (dom[0] <= L and U <= dom[1]):
        raise ValueError('the parameter machine runs only inside the valid write-back domain: the given interval leaves the valid domain')
    base = {}
    for path, d in zip(K, depths(G, o, K)):
        for (i, j) in path:
            base[(i, j)] = (o[0][i][j] - d) if rule == 'shift' else G.b[i][j]
    w = [[((base[(i, j)], F(1)) if (i, j) in base else (o[0][i][j], F(0))) for j in range(G.n_out)] for i in range(G.n_in)]
    v = [(F(vv), F(0)) for vv in o[1]]
    st = (w, v, list(o[2]), list(o[3]))
    out = []
    def concrete_from(st, t, c):
        wn = [[aff_eval(f, c) for f in row] for row in st[0]]
        vn = [aff_eval(f, c) for f in st[1]]
        _, rec = run_rec(G, (wn, vn, st[2], st[3]), hist[t:], plastic, bap)
        return rec
    def rec_(st, t, lo, hi, prefix):
        if t == len(hist):
            out.append(('open', lo, hi, prefix))
            return
        w, v, x, y = st
        name, I = hist[t]
        s = G.pat[name]
        I = [F(a) for a in I]
        vt = [(G.lam[j] * v[j][0] + sum(w[i][j][0] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j],
               G.lam[j] * v[j][1] + sum(w[i][j][1] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        comps = [(vt[j][0] - G.theta[j], vt[j][1]) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        # clipping comparisons need the gating first, so split by gating first
        roots = sorted(set(-f[0] / f[1] for f in comps if f[1] != 0 and lo < -f[0] / f[1] < hi))
        if roots:
            pts = [lo] + roots + [hi]
            for r in roots:
                rc = concrete_from(st, t, r)
                out.append(('point', r, r, prefix + rc))
            for a, b in zip(pts[:-1], pts[1:]):
                rec_(st, t, a, b, prefix)
            return
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        g = [1 if aff_eval(vt[j], mid) >= G.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(G.n_out)]
        v2 = [((G.vreset[j], F(0)) if g[j] else vt[j]) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        Ssum = [[None] * G.n_out for _ in range(G.n_in)]
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                pp = G.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)
                pm = -G.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)
                a0, a1 = w[i][j]
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    a0, a1 = a0 - G.rho * (a0 - G.b[i][j]), a1 - G.rho * a1
                Ssum[i][j] = (a0 + pp + pm, a1)
        croots = set()
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                f = Ssum[i][j]
                if f[1] != 0:
                    for bound in (G.lo[i][j], G.hi[i][j]):
                        r = (bound - f[0]) / f[1]
                        if lo < r < hi:
                            croots.add(r)
        if croots:
            croots = sorted(croots)
            pts = [lo] + croots + [hi]
            for r in croots:
                out.append(('point', r, r, prefix + concrete_from(st, t, r)))
            for a, b in zip(pts[:-1], pts[1:]):
                rec_(st, t, a, b, prefix)
            return
        w2 = [[None] * G.n_out for _ in range(G.n_in)]
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                f = Ssum[i][j]
                val = aff_eval(f, mid)
                if val > G.hi[i][j]:
                    w2[i][j] = (G.hi[i][j], F(0))
                elif val < G.lo[i][j]:
                    w2[i][j] = (G.lo[i][j], F(0))
                else:
                    w2[i][j] = f
        rec_((w2, v2, x2, y2), t + 1, lo, hi, prefix + [(tuple(s), tuple(g))])
    rec_(st, 0, L, U, [])
    return out

def param_B(G, o, K, hist, reading, plastic=True, bap=True, q=None, verify=2, rng=None, rule=None):
    """Pieces of B (or B^q) for c ∈ [min, max] ∩ ℚ (Lemma M1: exactly the range of c* over all valid m).
    First computes the valid write-back domain D = legal_domain; pieces outside D are recorded as 'undef-Im' (I_m undefined, no truth value); inside D the parameter machine gives exact pieces.
    Returns (Y0, list of pieces, number of check violations). Piece: (kind, a, b, val, Y1), kind ∈ {'point', 'open'}."""
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    lo, hi = min(ds), max(ds)
    _, rec0 = run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic, bap)
    Y0 = Ys(rec0, reading)
    dom = legal_domain(G, o, K, rule)
    pieces = []
    undef = {kk: 'undef-Im' for kk in Y0}
    def add_undef(kind, a, b):
        pieces.append((kind, a, b, dict(undef), None))
    if dom in (None, 'reject'):
        add_undef('point', lo, lo)
        if hi > lo:
            add_undef('open', lo, hi); add_undef('point', hi, hi)
        pieces.sort(key=lambda p: (p[1], 0 if p[0] == 'point' else 1))
        return Y0, pieces, 0
    L, U = dom
    if lo < L:
        add_undef('point', lo, lo); add_undef('open', lo, L)
    if U < hi:
        add_undef('open', U, hi); add_undef('point', hi, hi)
    if L == U:
        cand = [('point', L, L, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, L, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1])]
    else:
        cand = [('point', L, L, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, L, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1]),
                ('point', U, U, run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, U, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1])]
        cand += param_explore(G, o, K, hist, L, U, plastic, bap, rule)
    bad = 0
    rng = rng or random.Random(5)
    for kind, a, b, rec in cand:
        if kind == 'open':
            for _ in range(verify):
                c = a + (b - a) * F(rng.randint(1, 999), 1000)
                if run_rec(G, I_c(G, o, K, c, rule), hist, plastic, bap)[1] != rec:
                    bad += 1
        Y1 = Ys(rec, reading)
        val = {}
        for kk in Y0:
            val[kk] = B_value(Y0[kk], Y1[kk]) if q is None else Bq_value(kk, Y0[kk], Y1[kk], q)
        pieces.append((kind, a, b, val, Y1))
    pieces.sort(key=lambda p: (p[1], 0 if p[0] == 'point' else 1))
    return Y0, pieces, bad

def piece_at(pieces, c):
    for kind, a, b, val, Y1 in pieces:
        if (kind == 'point' and c == a) or (kind == 'open' and a < c < b):
            return kind, a, b, val, Y1
    return None

def summarize(pieces, key):
    vals = [p[3][key] for p in pieces]
    return dict(all_true=all(v is True for v in vals), all_false=all(v is False for v in vals),
                undef=any(v == 'undef-reading' for v in vals), undef_Im=any(v == 'undef-Im' for v in vals))

def show_pieces(pieces, keys, maxn=12):
    lines = []
    for kind, a, b, val, Y1 in pieces[:maxn]:
        rng_ = f'c*={a}' if kind == 'point' else f'c*∈({a}, {b})'
        lines.append('      ' + rng_ + ': ' + ('I_m undefined (write-back leaves the valid domain)' if Y1 is None else
                                         '; '.join(f'{k}: Y′={fmt(Y1[k])} B={val[k]}' for k in keys)))
    if len(pieces) > maxn:
        lines.append(f'      … {len(pieces)} pieces in all')
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# ---------- F, Ω_W, Bias ----------
def subsegs(n, k):
    sz = n // k
    return [(i * sz, (i + 1) * sz) for i in range(k)]

def FOB(G, o, K, hist, reading='fork', q=0, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Declared: k equal sub-segments; Pass_W(path q) = number of opening events; Familiar Route: z > 0 for every synapse in the path (Field Chapter 11, Section 3) and Pass ≥ k; the re-traversal criterion for Ω also takes Pass ≥ k,
    Brk is identically 0 in 𝒦_g (no disconnection mechanism; synapse identity persists, Chapter 11, 3.4 Ident), and the third item is that every sub-segment has an opening event;
    Bias: (4a) Depth_q − Depth_o > ε_δ at the start of W, (4b) in every sub-segment q has more opening events than the other path;
    F: Familiar Route ∧ in the comparison task T_k of every sub-segment (start state from the actual run, window = that sub-segment) Lead_q = sole lead ∧ B_m^q(T_k) true for every valid m (parameter machine)."""
    o_end, rec = run_rec(G, o, hist, plastic, bap)
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, reading)
    paths = sorted(cnt.keys(), key=str)
    qq, oo = paths[q], paths[1 - q]
    ds = depths(G, o, K)
    D = ds[0] != ds[1]
    fam = all(o[0][i][j] - G.b[i][j] > 0 for (i, j) in K[q]) and cnt[qq] >= k
    segs = subsegs(len(hist), k)
    seg_ok, seg_info = True, []
    cur = as_lists(o)
    for (a, b) in segs:
        sub = hist[a:b]
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(G, cur, K, sub, reading, plastic, bap, q=qq)
        ld = lead('first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share', Y0['first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share'], qq)
        kk = 'first' if reading != 'conv' else 'share'
        allB = all(p[3][kk] is True for p in pieces)
        seg_info.append((ld, allB, bad))
        seg_ok = seg_ok and ld == 'sole lead' and allB
        cur, _ = run_rec(G, cur, sub, plastic, bap)
    Fv = fam and seg_ok
    seg_pass = all(any(rec[t][1][qq if reading != 'conv' else 0] and all(rec[t][0][i] for i in A_IN) for t in range(a, b))
                   for (a, b) in segs) if reading == 'fork' else None
    Om = cnt[qq] >= k and seg_pass
    a4 = (ds[q] - ds[1 - q]) > eps_d
    def seg_cnt(a, b, p):
        _, c = times_counts(rec[a:b], reading)
        return c[p]
    b4 = all(seg_cnt(a, b, qq) > seg_cnt(a, b, oo) for (a, b) in segs)
    return dict(D=D, F=Fv, Omega=Om, Bias=a4 and b4, a4=a4, b4=b4, fam=fam, seg=seg_info, cnt=cnt)

# ---------- main program ----------
def main():
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    o26 = s1_state()
    print('0. Versions and reproduction')
    print('  snn_core.py', H_CORE[:16], '…; Chapter 11 gate_class.py', H_GATE[:16], '… (SHA-256 checked before loading)')
    print('  s1 declared history reproduced: formation-period log fingerprint 707404eb6302adc8…, W log fingerprint 84d320db9a654be6… (matching the fingerprints of the s1 frozen logs)')
    n, ncl, nti, bad = GC.compare_run(S, G, FORM + REST + WIN)
    print(f'  beat-by-beat check of Proposition 1 of Chapter 11 redone over the whole s1 history: {n} beats, mismatches {len(bad)}')
    print('  state at beat 26 (start of W): w =', [[str(c) for c in r] for r in o26[0]])
    print('                      v =', [str(c) for c in o26[1]], '; y =', [str(c) for c in o26[3]], '; x =', [str(c) for c in o26[2]])
    print('1. Correspondence of δ (Q1): z = w − b; δmax ↔ hi − b = 7/4; lower bound lo − b = −1/4 (z < 0 has no counterpart in Field Chapter 1; recorded)')
    o_end, rec = run_rec(G, o26, WIN)
    zmin = None
    oo = as_lists(o26)
    for sig in WIN:
        oo, _ = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
        zz = min(oo[0][i][j] - G.b[i][j] for i in range(4) for j in range(2))
        zmin = zz if zmin is None else min(zmin, zz)
    print('  minimum of z over all synapses within the s1 window W =', zmin, '(if < 0, recorded as "z < 0 position")')
    for K, nm in ((K_FORK, 'bifurcation'), (K_CONV, 'convergence'), (K_SINGLE, 'single synapse')):
        print(f'  {nm} K path depths (mean aggregation):', [str(d) for d in depths(G, o26, K)])
    print('2. Identity of threshold mechanisms and reduction of C₌ (Q2)')
    print('  bifurcation reading: the two outputs have the same λ = 3/4, θ = 1, reset 0; before firing, v_j(t) = λ^t v_j(0) + S_j·u_A(t) + R_j(t), S_j = w0j + w1j;')
    print('  under the symmetry condition SYM (v_j(0) equal, y_j(0) equal, non-K drive equal for the two outputs), the two paths share the entry drive u_A and the threshold order is given monotonically by S (Theorem S-FORK).')
    print('  s1 at beat 26: v =', [str(c) for c in o26[1]], ', y =', [str(c) for c in o26[3]], '— SYM fails (the shallower path has an initial membrane potential, the deeper path a residual postsynaptic trace),')
    print('  so by Form Chapter 20, Appendix 1, Section 1, "the required threshold order must be declared and verified directly":')
    tau, cnt = times_counts(rec, 'fork')
    print('    actual W: first opening beats (relative to the start of W)', tau, '; opening-event counts', cnt, '; direct reading of the P₁₇-type condition (gating argument of the shallower path < θ at the opening beat of the deeper path):',
          'gating argument of out1 at beat 26 =', str(F(3, 4) * o26[1][1] + o26[0][0][1] + o26[0][1][1]))
    print('3. I_m and existing interventions (Q3)')
    ds = depths(G, o26, K_FORK)
    o_min = I_c(G, o26, K_FORK, min(ds))
    abl = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3]))
    print('  whether "reset all weights to baseline at the start of W" and I_min(K_bif) give componentwise identical states:', abl == o_min,
          '; with I_min(K_conv):', abl == I_c(G, o26, K_CONV, min(depths(G, o26, K_CONV))))
    print('  (at beat 26 of s1 all non-K synapses are at baseline and the shallower path in K has depth 0 = min, so the two coincide numerically on s1; in general, when min_K Depth > 0, the reset violates the intermediacy condition)')
    print('  cutting off backpropagation, freezing plasticity: z on K is unchanged at the moment of intervention (only the process laws change); these are not I_m.')
    print('4. B_m on s1 (actual W, plasticity on; exact pieces as c* ranges over [min, max])')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    for K, reading, nm in ((K_FORK, 'fork', 'bifurcation'), (K_CONV, 'conv', 'convergence'), (K_SINGLE, 'single', 'single synapse')):
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rng=rng)
        keys = list(Y0.keys())
        print(f'  {nm}: original readings', '; '.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in keys), f'; pieces {len(pieces)}, random-point recheck violations {bad}')
        print(show_pieces(pieces, keys, maxn=25))
        for k in keys:
            print(f'    {k}: true for every valid m {summarize(pieces, k)["all_true"]}; false for every valid m {summarize(pieces, k)["all_false"]}; some reading undefined {summarize(pieces, k)["undef"]}')
        for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
            c = m(depths(G, o26, K))
            _, r1 = run_rec(G, I_c(G, o26, K, c), WIN)
            Y1 = Ys(r1, reading)
            print(f'    {m.__name__} (c* = {c}):', '; '.join(f'{k}: B={B_value(Y0[k], Y1[k])}' for k in keys))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_FORK, WIN, 'fork', q=1)
    print('  bifurcation B^q (q = shallower path 1) pieces:')
    print(show_pieces(pieces, ['first']))
    print('5. Write-back invariance of the convergence reading (Theorem S-INV): with the sum of the two synapses of each path fixed and no clipping within W, all events of output 0 are independent of how the write-back is split')
    nsplit = ndiff = nclip = 0
    for trial in range(200):
        c = F(rng.randint(0, 1000), 1000) * depths(G, o26, K_CONV)[0]
        base = I_c(G, o26, K_CONV, c)
        _, recb = run_rec(G, base, WIN)
        for path in K_CONV:
            (i1, j1), (i2, j2) = path
            delta = F(rng.randint(-500, 500), 1000) * F(1, 2)
            w = [list(r) for r in base[0]]
            w[i1][j1] += delta; w[i2][j2] -= delta
            if not all(G.lo[i][j] <= w[i][j] <= G.hi[i][j] for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
                continue
            o2 = (w, base[1], base[2], base[3])
            # check for clipping
            oo = as_lists(o2); clip = False
            for sig in WIN:
                oo, r = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
                clip = clip or any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for k in r['comp'])
            _, rec2 = run_rec(G, o2, WIN)
            nsplit += 1
            nclip += clip
            if not clip and [g for _, g in rec2] != [g for _, g in recb]:
                ndiff += 1
    print(f'  random splits {nsplit} (of which {nclip} with clipping within W); clipping-free splits whose event sequence differs from the R-uniform write-back {ndiff}')
    # witness that clipping can break the invariance: path-A sum = 3/4, even split (3/8, 3/8) vs uneven split (0, 3/4); at beat 26 the depression component is cut off by clipping at w00 = 0
    P3 = [('A', (0, 0))] * 3
    wa = [list(r) for r in o26[0]]; wa[0][0] = F(3, 8); wa[1][0] = F(3, 8)
    wb = [list(r) for r in o26[0]]; wb[0][0] = F(0); wb[1][0] = F(3, 4)
    _, r_a = run_rec(G, (wa, list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3])), P3)
    _, r_b = run_rec(G, (wb, list(o26[1]), list(o26[2]), list(o26[3])), P3)
    print('  clipping witness: beat-26 state, path-A sum = 3/4, splits (3/8, 3/8) and (0, 3/4), P = A×3: gating sequences', [g for _, g in r_a], 'vs', [g for _, g in r_b],
          '— on the uneven side the depression component is cut off by clipping at w00 = 0, the sums of the two splits separate, and whether output 0 fires in beat 2 differs')
    print('6. Disagreement between reading types (Q4)')
    # (a) s1 single-synapse K, m = max
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, 'single')
    c = max(depths(G, o26, K_SINGLE))
    _, r1 = run_rec(G, I_c(G, o26, K_SINGLE, c), WIN)
    Y1 = Ys(r1, 'single')
    print('  (a) s1, single-synapse K, m = max:', '; '.join(f'{k}: {fmt(Y0[k])} → {fmt(Y1[k])}, B={B_value(Y0[k], Y1[k])}' for k in Y0))
    # (b)(c) bifurcation contrasts under declared protocols (plasticity off, SYM holds)
    def mk(S0, S1, wB=F(1, 4)):
        w = [[S0 / 2, S1 / 2], [S0 / 2, S1 / 2], [wB, wB], [wB, wB]]
        return (w, [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)] * 4, [F(0), F(0)])
    P_b = [('A', (0, 0))] * 3
    ob_ = mk(F(3, 5), F(11, 20))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, ob_, K_FORK, P_b, 'fork', plastic=False)
    print('  (b) bifurcation, plasticity off, S₀ = 3/5, S₁ = 11/20 (split evenly between the two synapses of each path), non-K synapses 1/4, v = y = 0, P = A×3: original', '; '.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in Y0))
    print(show_pieces(pieces, list(Y0.keys())))
    P_c = [('AB', (0, 0))] + [('A', (0, 0))] * 5
    oc_ = mk(F(1), F(3, 5))
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, oc_, K_FORK, P_c, 'fork', plastic=False)
    print('  (c) bifurcation, plasticity off, S₀ = 1, S₁ = 3/5, non-K synapses 1/4, P = AB, A×5: original', '; '.join(f'{k}={fmt(Y0[k])}' for k in Y0))
    print(show_pieces(pieces, list(Y0.keys())))
    print('7. Finite check of Theorem S-FORK (random 𝒦_g members, ρ ∈ {0, 1/10, 1/3, 1/2, 1}, random plastic and bap (ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]), SYM states (possibly uneven within paths), random protocols; R-uniform write-back;')
    print('    first-opened reading: B ⇔ the original reading is a singleton, independently of m; cases with D = 0 included)')
    nn = viol = skip = badtot = nD0 = 0
    n_eq = v_eq = 0
    rng2 = random.Random(37)
    perrho = {}
    for trial in range(400):
        lam = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        th = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2)])
        d = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Ap = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)]); Am = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        full = lambda val: [[val] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Gx = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [lam] * 2, [th] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [d] * 4, [d] * 2, Ap, Am, rho, full(F(1, 4)), full(F(0)), full(F(2)))
        S0 = F(rng.randint(2, 30), 20); S1 = rng.choice([S0, F(rng.randint(2, 30), 20)])
        u0, u1 = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        wB2, wB3 = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        w = [[S0 / 2 + u0, S1 / 2 + u0], [S0 / 2 - u0, S1 / 2 - u0], [wB2, wB2], [wB3, wB3]]
        if not all(0 <= w[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        vv = F(rng.randint(0, 9), 10); yy = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(4)], [yy, yy])
        L = rng.randint(2, 8)
        P = [(rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([0, 0, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(L)]
        plastic = rng.choice([True, False])
        bapv = rng.choice([True, False])
        _, rec0 = run_rec(Gx, o, P, plastic, bapv)
        # antecedent P-A: no firing on a non-entry beat before the first opening event
        first = None
        okA = True
        for t, (s, g) in enumerate(rec0):
            if any(g):
                if all(s[i] for i in A_IN):
                    break
                okA = False
                break
        if not okA:
            skip += 1
            continue
        # antecedent NC: no clipping event in K before the first opening event (when the two synapses of a path are unequal, clipping can reverse the order of the path sums; see the antecedents of Theorem S-FORK in the main text)
        oo = as_lists(o); clipK = False
        for sig in P:
            oo, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bapv)
            clipK = clipK or any(r_['comp'][k][3] != r_['comp'][k][4] for path in K_FORK for k in path)
            if any(r_['g']):
                break
        if clipK:
            skip += 1
            continue
        Y0, pieces, bad = param_B(Gx, o, K_FORK, P, 'fork', plastic=plastic, bap=bapv, verify=1, rng=rng, rule='uniform')
        badtot += bad
        # reading equivalence: the valid write-back R-shift and the auxiliary operation R-uniform give the same Y_first when c ∈ C_leg (R-shift) and neither intervention run has clipping in K before its first opening event (NC′)
        dom_s = legal_domain(Gx, o, K_FORK, 'shift')
        if dom_s not in (None, 'reject'):
            for c in {dom_s[0], dom_s[1], dom_s[0] + (dom_s[1] - dom_s[0]) * F(rng2.randint(1, 99), 100)}:
                ys, ncp = [], True
                for rl in ('uniform', 'shift'):
                    oi = I_c(Gx, o, K_FORK, c, rl)
                    oo = as_lists(oi); rec_i = []
                    for sig in P:
                        oo, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])), plastic=plastic, bap=bapv)
                        rec_i.append((tuple(r_['s']), tuple(r_['g'])))
                        if not any(r_['g'][j] and all(r_['s'][i] for i in A_IN) for j in (0, 1)) and \
                                any(r_['comp'][k][3] != r_['comp'][k][4] for path in K_FORK for k in path):
                            ncp = False
                        if any(r_['g'][j] and all(r_['s'][i] for i in A_IN) for j in (0, 1)):
                            break
                    ys.append(Ys(run_rec(Gx, oi, P, plastic, bapv)[1], 'fork')['first'])
                if ncp:
                    n_eq += 1
                    v_eq += (ys[0] != ys[1])
        single = len(Y0['first']) == 1
        vals = set(p[3]['first'] for p in pieces)
        nn += 1
        keyc = ('p' if plastic else '-') + ('b' if bapv else '-')
        perrho[keyc] = perrho.get(keyc, 0) + 1
        if S0 == S1:
            nD0 += 1
        viol += not (len(vals) == 1 and vals.pop() == single)
    print(f'  valid cases {nn} (by plastic/bap: {perrho}; of which with D = 0 {nD0}) (skipped because antecedent P-A or NC fails {skip}); violations {viol}; parameter-machine random-point recheck violations {badtot}')
    print(f'  reading equivalence (valid write-back R-shift vs auxiliary operation R-uniform; c at both ends and one interior point of the valid domain of R-shift; cases where NC′ holds): comparisons {n_eq}, first-opened readings differing {v_eq}')
    print('8. F, Ω_W, Bias (bifurcation reading, q = path 0, 3 sub-segments × 6 beats, ε_δ = 1/100; B^q in F evaluated for every valid m)')
    for plastic, nm in ((True, 's1 actual (plasticity on)'), (False, 's1 fixed weights (plasticity off: no deepening action)')):
        r = FOB(G, o26, K_FORK, WIN, plastic=plastic)
        print(f'  {nm}: D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]} (4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}) Familiar Route={r["fam"]} opening events {r["cnt"]}; sub-segments (Lead, ∀m B^q, recheck violations) {r["seg"]}')
        if not plastic:
            _, rr = run_rec(G, o26, WIN, plastic=False)
            print('    in the fixed-weight run z on K stays unchanged throughout (no Dτ component):', as_lists(o26)[0] == run_rec(G, o26, WIN, plastic=False)[0][0])
    o_eq = I_c(G, o26, K_FORK, max(depths(G, o26, K_FORK)))
    r = FOB(G, o_eq, K_FORK, WIN)
    print(f'  equal-depth state (I_max applied at beat 26, taken as the new start): D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]} opening events {r["cnt"]}')
    r = FOB(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, reading='single')
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_SINGLE, WIN, 'single')
    print(f'  single-synapse K: D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Bias={r["Bias"]} (4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}); B at c* = min:', pieces[0][3])
    W1 = [('A', (0, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 17
    r = FOB(G, o26, K_FORK, W1)
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o26, K_FORK, W1, 'fork')
    print(f'  single input (A, then rest for 17 beats): D={r["D"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}; B^first true for every valid m {summarize(pieces, "first")["all_true"]}')
    print('9. Representation requirement (Q6, Theorem R-B): identical actual witness histories with different B_m')
    th_list = [F(1), F(3)]
    recs, Bs, Fs = [], [], []
    for th1 in th_list:
        Gx = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1), th1], [F(0)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(0),
                     [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        o, recA = run_rec(Gx, Gx.init(), FORM + REST)
        _, recW = run_rec(Gx, o, WIN)
        full_hist = []
        oo = Gx.init()
        for sig in FORM + REST + WIN:
            oo2, r_ = Gx.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
            full_hist.append((r_['g'], tuple(sorted((k, v[4]) for k, v in r_['comp'].items())), tuple(oo2[1]), tuple(oo2[2]), tuple(oo2[3])))
            oo = oo2
        recs.append(full_hist)
        Y0 = Ys(recW, 'fork')
        c = max(depths(Gx, o, K_FORK))
        _, r1 = run_rec(Gx, I_c(Gx, o, K_FORK, c), WIN)
        Bs.append(B_value(Y0['first'], Ys(r1, 'fork')['first']))
        # direct per-sub-segment computation of F (not inferred from the whole-window B)
        cur = as_lists(o)
        segrows = []
        for (a, b) in subsegs(len(WIN), 3):
            sub = WIN[a:b]
            _, rk = run_rec(Gx, cur, sub)
            Yk = Ys(rk, 'fork')
            cm = max(depths(Gx, cur, K_FORK))
            _, rkI = run_rec(Gx, I_c(Gx, cur, K_FORK, cm), sub)
            bq_max = Bq_value('first', Yk['first'], Ys(rkI, 'fork')['first'], 0)
            _, pcs, _ = param_B(Gx, cur, K_FORK, sub, 'fork', q=0)
            segrows.append((lead('first', Yk['first'], 0), bq_max, all(pp[3]['first'] is True for pp in pcs)))
            cur, _ = run_rec(Gx, cur, sub)
        Fs.append((segrows, FOB(Gx, o, K_FORK, WIN)['F']))
    print('  G (θ₁ = 1) and G′ (θ₁ = 3): the comparison specifications agree except for M (K_bif, P = s1 declared history, W, first-opened set, m = max);')
    print('  over the whole history from the initial state (formation, rest, W: 44 beats in all), the gating, the post-update value of each synapse, v, x and y are identical at every beat:', recs[0] == recs[1])
    print('  whole-window B_max^first: G =', Bs[0], '; G′ =', Bs[1])
    print('  per sub-segment (Lead₀, B^q for m = max, B^q for every valid m): G =', Fs[0][0], '; G′ =', Fs[1][0])
    print('  F(path 0;T,W) (for-every-valid-m convention): G =', Fs[0][1], '; G′ =', Fs[1][1])
    print('10. Degeneration mechanisms')
    Gd = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 16), F(1, 16), F(0),
                 [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
    od, _ = run_rec(Gd, Gd.init(), FORM + REST)
    print('  (1) potentiation = depression (A₊ = A₋ = 1/16): after the s1 formation history z_T =', [str(od[0][i][0] - F(1, 4)) for i in (0, 1)],
          '; bifurcation K path depths', [str(d) for d in depths(Gd, od, K_FORK)], '→ D is false, so B_m and F are false by Proposition 1 and F ⇒ B')
    cnt_ok = cnt_all = 0
    for trial in range(300):
        dd = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        A = rng.choice([F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        Gp = GC.Gate(4, 2, S.PAT, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [F(0)] * 2, [dd] * 4, [dd] * 2, A, A, F(0),
                     [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        L = rng.randint(1, 15)
        P = [rng.choice([('A', (F(1), F(0))), ('none', (0, 0))]) for _ in range(L)]   # teaching current 1 makes output 0 fire in the same beat as pattern A
        o, rec_ = run_rec(Gp, Gp.init(), P)
        cof = all((s[0] == g[0]) for s, g in rec_)
        if cof:
            cnt_all += 1
            cnt_ok += all(o[0][i][0] == F(1, 4) for i in (0, 1))
    print(f'    finite check of Proposition DG-1 (d_x = d_y, pre and post strictly in the same beat, initial traces 0): cases {cnt_all}, cases with w₀₀, w₁₀ kept at baseline {cnt_ok}')
    # (2) MINUS reads the weight through gating
    o = as_lists(o26)
    oA = I_c(G, o, [((0, 0),)], F(0)); oB = o
    _, rA = G.step(as_lists(oA), ('A', (F(0), F(0))))
    _, rB = G.step(as_lists(oB), ('A', (F(0), F(0))))
    print('  (2) the depression component reads the weight through gating: at beat 26 only w₀₀ is changed (z = 0 and z = 0.418…); depression component P⁻ of input 0→output 0 =',
          str(rA['comp'][(0, 0)][1]), 'and', str(rB['comp'][(0, 0)][1]), '(g₀ in the same beat is determined by w; y′₀ = d·y₀ + g₀)')
    # (3) clipping and δmax: saturation makes D vanish within W
    o_sat = ([[F(1, 4) + F(6, 5), F(1, 4) + F(9, 10)], [F(1, 4) + F(6, 5), F(1, 4) + F(9, 10)], [F(1, 4)] * 2, [F(1, 4)] * 2],
             [F(0), F(0)], [F(0)] * 4, [F(0), F(0)])
    P = [('A', (0, 0))] * 30
    oe, rec_ = run_rec(G, o_sat, P)
    print('  (3) clipping: bifurcation path depths at the start', [str(d) for d in depths(G, o_sat, K_FORK)], ', path depths after A×30 (plasticity on)', [str(d) for d in depths(G, oe, K_FORK)],
          '— both paths reach δmax = 7/4 and D vanishes')
    Y0, pieces, _ = param_B(G, o_sat, K_FORK, P, 'fork')
    print('    on this window, B for the first-opened reading false for every valid m:', summarize(pieces, 'first')['all_false'], '; B for the share reading true for every valid m:',
          summarize(pieces, 'share')['all_true'])
    print('11. Effect of the write-back rule on the s1 results (R-uniform and R-shift)')
    oo = as_lists(o26)
    unif = []
    for t, sig in enumerate(WIN):
        if t % 6 == 0:
            unif.append((26 + t, oo[0][0][0] == oo[0][1][0], oo[0][0][1] == oo[0][1][1], oo[0][2][0] == oo[0][3][0]))
        oo, _ = G.step(oo, (sig[0], tuple(F(v) for v in sig[1])))
    print('  within-path uniformity at the intervention points of the actual run (start of W and starts of sub-segments) (beat, w00 = w10, w01 = w11, w20 = w30):', unif)
    for K, reading, nm in ((K_FORK, 'fork', 'bifurcation'), (K_CONV, 'conv', 'convergence'), (K_SINGLE, 'single', 'single synapse')):
        _, pu, _ = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rule='uniform')
        _, ps, _ = param_B(G, o26, K, WIN, reading, rule='shift')
        same = [(a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3]) for a in pu] == [(a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3]) for a in ps]
        print(f'  {nm}: all pieces and values identical item by item under R-uniform and R-shift {same}')
    print('  Conclusion: s1 is uniform within paths at every intervention point, so R-uniform = R-shift, and none of the s1 readings depends on the choice of write-back rule.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9  regress12.py  Boundary examples and targeted checks: Theorem AGG, the convolution formula and the effective relaxation rate, Proposition B-D, the valid parameter domain, the antecedents of Theorem S-INV, reading equivalence, Lemma C-REP (10.1, 10.2, 10.3, 10.6, 10.9, 11.1, 11.7)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Sedimentation-Dynamics Concretization: boundary examples and targeted checks — the two kinds of criterion for uniqueness of the write-back set, the S-FORK convolution formula and the effective relaxation rate, equal-depth identity and B ⇒ D, the valid parameter domain of R-shift,
the antecedents of Theorem S-INV (non-overlapping paths, above-baseline amounts), reading equivalence, and the formal C₌-equivalent representation of the bifurcation reading.
Run: python3 regress12.py
Dependency: gate_class.py (Appendix A.2, SHA-256 checked before loading). Standard library only; exact rational arithmetic.
"""
import sys, os, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from deps12 import GC
import agg12 as AG
Z = F(0)

def u_rho(opens, lam, rho):
    """u_A^ρ(t) = Σ_{s=t₀}^{t} λ^{t−s}(1−ρ)^{s−t₀}[A_in open at beat s] (0⁰ = 1). opens gives open/closed for each beat t₀…t. Callers pass ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]."""
    t = len(opens) - 1
    return sum((lam ** (t - s) * (1 - rho) ** s for s in range(t + 1) if opens[s]), Z)

def gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, [lam] * n_out, [theta] * n_out, [Z] * n_out, [dx] * n_in, [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, rho,
                   [[b] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[lo] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[hi] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)])

def first_open(G, recs, A_in):
    for t, r in enumerate(recs):
        if all(r['s'][i] for i in A_in):
            opened = frozenset(j for j in range(G.n_out) if r['g'][j])
            if opened:
                return opened
    return frozenset()

def run(G, o, hist):
    recs = []
    for sig in hist:
        o, r = G.step(o, sig)
        recs.append(r)
    return o, recs

def rd01():
    print('1. Uniqueness of the write-back set (Theorem AGG)')
    # (1) linear: box [0,1]^3, A = [(1,0,0),(0,1/2,1/2)], c* = 0
    A = [[F(1), Z, Z], [Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]]
    r = AG.linear_writeback(A, F(0), [F(0)] * 3, [F(1)] * 3)
    print(f'  (1) linear boundary example: rank A = {AG.rank(A)} < 3; coordinate projections of the write-back set {r["proj"]}; solvable {r["feasible"]}, unique {r["unique"]}; '
          f'the rank criterion alone (rank = |X_K|) gives unique = {AG.rank(A) == 3} (mismatch); the feasible-direction criterion gives unique = {r["cone_unique"]}')
    # (3) bottleneck: K = {{x},{y},{x,y}}, c* = 1/2, box [0,1]^2
    paths = [(0,), (1,), (0, 1)]
    for c in (F(1, 2), F(1)):
        u = AG.bottleneck_unique(paths, 2, c, F(1))
        b = AG.bottleneck_unique_brute(paths, 2, c, F(1))
        print(f'  (3) bottleneck boundary example K = {{x}},{{y}},{{x,y}}, c* = {c}, h = 1: unique by path-wise criterion = {u}; unique by exhaustive subset search = {b}; "a path of length ≥ 2 means non-unique" gives non-unique (mismatch)')
    print('  finite check of the repaired criteria:')
    n, viol, nint, vint = AG.random_linear_check(2000, seed=20261102)
    print(f'    linear: random A, c*, box, {n} cases; "the write-back set is a single point" (coordinate-wise Fourier–Motzkin projection) agrees with "ker A ∩ tangent cone of the box = {{0}}", violations {viol}; '
          f'on the {nint} of these with c* strictly inside the box and the uniform solution feasible, agreement with "rank A = |X_K|", violations {vint}')
    n, viol = AG.random_bottleneck_check(3000, seed=7)
    print(f'    bottleneck: random path families, {n} cases; "path-wise criterion" agrees with "exhaustive search over subsets of raised positions", violations {viol}')

def rd02():
    print('2. The S-FORK convolution formula (effective relaxation rate ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic])')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, Z, F(2), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    o = ([[F(1), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
    o1, r1 = G.step(o, ('none', (Z, Z)))
    o2, r2 = G.step(o1, ('A', (Z, Z)))
    dvt = r2['vt'][0] - r2['vt'][1]
    E0 = o[0][0][0] - o[0][0][1]
    old = E0 * 1                                 # formula ignoring relaxation: u_A = Σ λ^{t−s}[A open] = 1
    new = E0 * u_rho([0, 1], Z, F(1, 2))      # convolution formula: u_A^ρ = Σ λ^{t−s}(1−ρ_eff)^{s−t₀}[A open]
    print(f'  boundary example (ρ = 1/2, plastic on): weights after rest {[str(w) for w in o1[0][0]]}; vt difference in the second beat (measured) = {dvt}; formula ignoring relaxation = {old} (mismatch); convolution formula = {new}; gating {r1["g"]}, {r2["g"]}')
    print('  targeted regression: on prefixes with random SYM states (uneven within paths included), ENT, no gating event and no clipping, with random plastic and bap, check vt₀ − vt₁ = E(t₀)·u_A^{ρ_eff}(t) beat by beat, ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic]')
    rng = random.Random(11)
    tot = viol = 0
    per = {}
    wrong_hits = [0]
    for trial in range(1500):
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 3), F(3, 4), F(1, 10)])
        lam = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        d = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)])
        Am = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16), F(1, 8)])
        G = gate(3, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0)}, lam, F(100), d, d, F(1, 8), Am, rho,
                 F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
        w = [[F(rng.randint(5, 35), 20) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(2)]
        wb = F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20)
        w.append([wb, wb])
        vv, yy = F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10), F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4) for _ in range(3)], [yy, yy])
        E0 = (w[0][0] + w[1][0]) - (w[0][1] + w[1][1])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        rho_eff = rho if plastic else Z
        L = rng.randint(1, 8)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(L)]
        opens = []
        for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
            o, r = G.step(o, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            opens.append(1 if all(r['s'][i] for i in (0, 1)) else 0)
            if any(r['g']) or any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for k in r['comp']):
                break
            pred = E0 * u_rho(opens, lam, rho_eff)
            ok = (r['vt'][0] - r['vt'][1]) == pred
            tot += 1; viol += (not ok)
            key = (('p' if plastic else '-') + ('b' if bap else '-'), str(rho))
            per[key] = per.get(key, 0) + 1
            # contrast: the formula that misuses ρ (without the factor [plastic])
            if not plastic and rho != 0 and E0 != 0 and sum(opens[1:]) > 0:
                wrong_hits[0] += ((r['vt'][0] - r['vt'][1]) != E0 * u_rho(opens, lam, rho))
    byc = {}
    for (c, r), n in per.items():
        byc[c] = byc.get(c, 0) + n
    print(f'    beats {tot} (by plastic/bap: {byc}; ρ values {sorted(set(r for _, r in per))}, every group occurs), violations {viol}')
    print(f'    contrast: with plastic off and ρ ≠ 0, beats where ρ used in place of ρ_eff disagrees with the measurement {wrong_hits[0]}')
    print('  endpoint: at ρ = 1, relaxation in the first beat already erases the path-depth difference, and only the s = t₀ term of u_A^ρ remains:')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for hist in ([('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))], [('A', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]):
        o = ([[F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
        _, recs = run(G, o, hist)
        print(f'    weights (3/4, 1/2), history {[h[0] for h in hist]}: vt difference per beat {[str(r["vt"][0] - r["vt"][1]) for r in recs]}')

def rd03():
    print('3. Equal depth but uneven within paths: B ⇒ D fails under R-uniform and holds under R-shift, which is the identity at equal depth')
    G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, Z, F(1, 4), F(1), F(1), Z, F(1, 2), Z, F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    w = [[Z, F(1, 2)], [F(1), F(1, 2)]]
    o = (w, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)])
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
    dep = [sum((w[i][j] - F(1, 4) for (i, j) in p), Z) / 2 for p in K]
    hist = [('A', (F(-2), F(-2))), ('A', (Z, Z))]
    _, r0 = run(G, o, hist)
    Y0 = first_open(G, r0, (0, 1))
    c = dep[0]
    wu = [[F(1, 4) + c] * 2 for _ in range(2)]                     # R-uniform
    _, ru = run(G, (wu, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)]), hist)
    Yu = first_open(G, ru, (0, 1))
    ws = [[w[i][j] + (c - dep[[0, 1][j]]) for j in range(2)] for i in range(2)]   # R-shift: every position in a path shifts by the same amount
    _, rs = run(G, (ws, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [F(1), F(1)]), hist)
    Ys = first_open(G, rs, (0, 1))
    print(f'  boundary example: path depths {[str(x) for x in dep]} (D = 0), c* = {c}')
    print(f'    actual gating {[r["g"] for r in r0]}, Y = {set(Y0)}; gating after the R-uniform write-back {[[str(x) for x in r] for r in wu]}: {[r["g"] for r in ru]}, Y′ = {set(Yu)}, B = {Y0 != Yu} (B ⇒ D fails)')
    print(f'    clipping in the first beat: pre-clip sum of w00 in the actual run = {r0[0]["comp"][(0, 0)][3]} (< lo = 0, cut off) — the S-INV antecedent "no clipping" fails')
    print(f'    gating after the R-shift write-back {[[str(x) for x in r] for r in ws]} (the identity at equal depth): {[r["g"] for r in rs]}, Y′ = {set(Ys)}, B = {Y0 != Ys}')
    print('  targeted regression: random 𝒦_g, random states equal in depth but uneven within paths, random protocols (clipping included):')
    rng = random.Random(3)
    n = viol_shift = fail_u = fail_u_noclip = 0
    for trial in range(3000):
        lam = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])
        G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, lam, rng.choice([F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]),
                 rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 8)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 4), F(1, 2)]),
                 rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
        s = F(rng.randint(0, 30), 20)                    # synapse sum of each path
        a0, a1 = F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20), F(rng.randint(0, 20), 20)
        if not (0 <= a0 <= min(s, 2) and 0 <= s - a0 <= 2 and 0 <= a1 <= min(s, 2) and 0 <= s - a1 <= 2):
            continue
        w = [[a0, a1], [s - a0, s - a1]]
        y = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)
        o = (w, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [y, y])
        hist = [('A' if rng.random() < .7 else 'none', (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(-1), F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 5))]
        _, r0 = run(G, o, hist)
        Y0 = first_open(G, r0, (0, 1))
        c = s / 2 - F(1, 4)
        wu = [[F(1, 4) + c] * 2 for _ in range(2)]
        _, ru = run(G, (wu, [Z, Z], [Z, Z], [y, y]), hist)
        clip = any(r['comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for r in r0 + ru for k in r['comp'])
        n += 1
        if first_open(G, ru, (0, 1)) != Y0:
            fail_u += 1
            fail_u_noclip += (not clip)
        # R-shift: the write-back is the identity at equal depth, so the rerun equals the actual run
        _, rs = run(G, o, hist)
        viol_shift += (first_open(G, rs, (0, 1)) != Y0)
    print(f'    cases {n}: B ⇒ D fails under R-uniform in {fail_u} cases (of which {fail_u_noclip} with no clipping in either run; should be 0: S-INV); failures under R-shift {viol_shift}')

def rd04():
    print('4. Relaxation does not run when plasticity is off (ρ_eff = ρ·[plastic])')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, Z, F(2), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1, 2), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for plastic in (True, False):
        for bap in (True, False):
            o = ([[F(1), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
            o1, r1 = G.step(o, ('none', (Z, Z)), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            o2, r2 = G.step(o1, ('A', (Z, Z)), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            rho_eff = F(1, 2) if plastic else Z
            print(f'  plastic={plastic}, bap={bap}: weights after rest {[str(x) for x in o1[0][0]]}; vt difference in the second beat (measured) {r2["vt"][0] - r2["vt"][1]}; '
                  f'ρ_eff formula {F(1, 2) * u_rho([0, 1], Z, rho_eff)}; formula with ρ in place of ρ_eff {F(1, 2) * u_rho([0, 1], Z, F(1, 2))}')
    print('  the antecedent of the endpoint "ρ = 1 levels in the first beat" must include plastic = True (bap irrelevant):')
    G = gate(1, 2, {'A': (1,), 'none': (0,)}, F(1, 2), F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, F(1), F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    for plastic, bap in ((True, True), (True, False), (False, True)):
        o = ([[F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]], [Z, Z], [Z], [Z, Z])
        oo, rr = o, []
        for sig in [('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]:
            oo, r = G.step(oo, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            rr.append(r)
        print(f'    ρ = 1, weights (3/4, 1/2), history none, A, plastic={plastic}, bap={bap}: vt difference per beat {[str(r["vt"][0] - r["vt"][1]) for r in rr]}')

def rd05():
    print('5. The valid parameter domain of R-shift')
    import snn12 as N
    G = GC.snn_gate(N.S)
    def st(w00, w10):
        w = [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        w[0][0], w[1][0] = w00, w10
        return (w, [Z, Z], [Z] * 4, [Z, Z])
    H1 = [('A', (0, 0))]
    o = st(Z, F(2))
    dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, N.K_FORK, 'shift')
    print(f'  degenerate case: w00 = 0, w10 = 2, path depths {[str(d) for d in N.depths(G, o, N.K_FORK)]}; valid domain of R-shift ∩ [min, max] = {dom}')
    for c in (Z, F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        print(f'    c = {c}: I_c defined {N.I_c(G, o, N.K_FORK, c, "shift") is not None}')
    try:
        N.param_explore(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1, Z, F(3, 4), rule='shift')
        print('    parameter machine ran on (0, 3/4) (error)')
    except ValueError as e:
        print('    parameter machine refuses to run on (0, 3/4):', e)
    Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1, 'fork', rule='shift')
    print('    param_B pieces:', [(k, str(a), str(b), 'undef-Im' if y is None else 'defined') for k, a, b, v, y in pcs])
    o = st(F(1, 2), F(3, 2))
    dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, N.K_FORK, 'shift')
    Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, N.K_FORK, H1 * 3, 'fork', rule='shift')
    print(f'  non-degenerate valid domain: w00 = 1/2, w10 = 3/2, path depths {[str(d) for d in N.depths(G, o, N.K_FORK)]}, valid domain {tuple(str(x) for x in dom)}')
    for c in (Z, F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        pc = N.piece_at(pcs, c)
        print(f'    c = {c}: I_c defined {N.I_c(G, o, N.K_FORK, c, "shift") is not None}; parameter-machine piece {pc[0]}, {"undef-Im" if pc[4] is None else "B=" + str(pc[3]["first"])}')
    print(f'    m valid as a function but I_m undefined: m = min (c* = 0); m = max (c* = 3/4) is defined')
    K_sh = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((0, 0),)]
    print(f'  shared Structural Positions: K = {{path A, input-0 branch}}, R-shift: valid domain = {N.legal_domain(G, o, K_sh, "shift")}; I_c(1/4) defined {N.I_c(G, o, K_sh, F(1, 4), "shift") is not None}; '
          f'param_B all undef-Im {all(p[4] is None for p in N.param_B(G, o, K_sh, H1, "fork", rule="shift")[1])}')
    print('  randomized regression: random states uneven within paths, random short histories; at min, max, both ends of the valid domain, a random point inside it and the points just outside it, compare the definedness of I_c with the parameter-machine pieces, and compare the records at defined points')
    rng = random.Random(23)
    n = viol = notall = empty = pts = 0
    for trial in range(400):
        w = [[F(rng.randint(0, 40), 20) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        o = (w, [F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10)] * 2, [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4) for _ in range(4)], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2)
        K = rng.choice([N.K_FORK, N.K_CONV])
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (0, 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
        rd = 'fork' if K is N.K_FORK else 'conv'
        Y0, pcs, bad = N.param_B(G, o, K, hist, rd, rule='shift', verify=1, rng=rng)
        viol += bad
        ds = N.depths(G, o, K); lo, hi = min(ds), max(ds)
        dom = N.legal_domain(G, o, K, 'shift')
        n += 1
        if dom is None:
            empty += 1
        elif not (dom[0] == lo and dom[1] == hi):
            notall += 1
        cs = {lo, hi}
        if dom not in (None, 'reject'):
            cs |= {dom[0], dom[1], dom[0] + (dom[1] - dom[0]) * F(rng.randint(1, 99), 100)}
            for e in (dom[0] - F(1, 1000), dom[1] + F(1, 1000)):
                if lo <= e <= hi:
                    cs.add(e)
        for c in cs:
            pts += 1
            pc = N.piece_at(pcs, c)
            Ic = N.I_c(G, o, K, c, 'shift')
            if pc is None or ((pc[4] is None) != (Ic is None)):
                viol += 1
            elif Ic is not None and N.Ys(N.run_rec(G, Ic, hist)[1], rd) != pc[4]:
                viol += 1
    print(f'    states {n}, check points {pts}; empty valid domain {empty}, valid domain a proper subset of [min, max] (some valid m leaves I_m undefined) {notall}; mismatches {viol}')

def rd06():
    print('6. Antecedents of Theorem S-INV: non-overlapping paths, only these Structural Positions changed, the rest of the start state the same; differences of above-baseline amounts shrink by the factor (1 − ρ_eff)')
    # overlapping entry groups: equal path sums but different gating (excluded by the "non-overlapping paths" antecedent)
    G = GC.Gate(3, 1, {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'A': (1, 1, 1)}, [Z], [F(8, 5)], [Z], [F(1, 2)] * 3, [F(1, 2)], Z, Z, Z,
                [[Z] for _ in range(3)], [[Z] for _ in range(3)], [[F(2)] for _ in range(3)])
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((1, 0), (2, 0))]
    a = G.init(w=[[F(1, 2)], [F(1, 2)], [F(1, 2)]])
    b = G.init(w=[[F(3, 4)], [F(1, 4)], [F(3, 4)]])
    sums = [sum((o[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) for p in K for o in (a, b)]
    _, ra = G.step(a, ('A', (Z,)), plastic=False)
    _, rb = G.step(b, ('A', (Z,)), plastic=False)
    print(f'  overlapping entry groups (both paths use w10): path sums of the two states {[str(x) for x in sums]}; gating arguments {ra["vt"][0]} and {rb["vt"][0]}, gating {ra["g"]} and {rb["g"]} — "non-overlapping paths" fails, and the conclusion does not hold')
    # different baselines within a group: the weight difference does not shrink proportionally, the difference of above-baseline amounts does
    G = GC.Gate(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'A': (1, 1)}, [Z], [F(10)], [Z], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], Z, Z, F(1, 2),
                [[F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2)]], [[Z]] * 2, [[F(2)]] * 2)
    o = G.init(w=[[F(1)], [F(1)]])
    o2, _ = G.step(o, ('none', (Z,)))
    dw0, dw1 = o[0][0][0] - o[0][1][0], o2[0][0][0] - o2[0][1][0]
    de0 = (o[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]) - (o[0][1][0] - G.b[1][0])
    de1 = (o2[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]) - (o2[0][1][0] - G.b[1][0])
    print(f'  baselines within the group (1/4, 1/2), weights (1, 1), ρ = 1/2, plastic on: weight difference {dw0} → {dw1}; difference of above-baseline amounts {de0} → {de1} (= (1 − ρ_eff)·{de0})')
    # randomized check: non-overlapping entry-group paths, baselines may differ within a group, plastic × bap × ρ
    rng = random.Random(29)
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    K = [((0, 0), (1, 0)), ((2, 0), (3, 0)), ((0, 1), (1, 1))]
    n = viol = clipped = 0
    per = {}
    for trial in range(1500):
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 10), F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 8), 16) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(4, 2, pat, [rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2), F(3, 4)])] * 2, [rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)])] * 2, [Z] * 2,
                    [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, rng.choice([Z, F(1, 8)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16)]), rho, b,
                    [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
        w = [[F(rng.randint(4, 28), 16) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        w2 = [r[:] for r in w]
        for path in K:
            (i1, j1), (i2, j2) = path
            d = F(rng.randint(-6, 6), 16)
            w2[i1][j1] += d; w2[i2][j2] -= d
        if not all(0 <= w2[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(4) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        x = [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2 + [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2
        common = ([F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8)] * 2, x, [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2)
        oa, ob = (w, *common), (w2, *[list(c) for c in common])
        hist = [(rng.choice(list(pat)), (rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]), rng.choice([Z, F(1, 2)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 6))]
        clip = False
        same = True
        for sig in hist:
            oa, r1 = G.step(oa, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            ob, r2 = G.step(ob, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            clip = clip or any(r[ 'comp'][k][3] != r['comp'][k][4] for r in (r1, r2) for path in K for k in path)
            same = same and r1['g'] == r2['g'] and oa[1:] == ob[1:] and \
                all(sum((oa[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) == sum((ob[0][i][j] for i, j in p), Z) for p in K)
        if clip:
            clipped += 1
            continue
        n += 1
        key = ('plastic' if plastic else '-') + '/' + ('bap' if bap else '-') + '/' + ('ρ_eff>0' if plastic and rho else 'ρ_eff=0')
        per[key] = per.get(key, 0) + 1
        viol += (not same)
    print(f'  randomized check: {n} cases with non-overlapping paths (differing baselines within a group included), only K changed, the rest of the start state the same, and no clipping (by switches and ρ_eff: {per}; omitted because of clipping {clipped}); cases where gating, v, x, y or the path sums disagree at some beat {viol}')

def rd07():
    print('7. Equal-depth identity, auxiliary operations and reading equivalence')
    G = GC.Gate(2, 2, {'none': (0, 0), 'A': (1, 1)}, [Z] * 2, [F(1)] * 2, [Z] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, Z, Z, Z,
                [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(2)], [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(2)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(2)])
    a = G.init(w=[[F(1, 4), F(1, 2)], [F(3, 4), F(1, 2)]])
    b = G.init(w=[[F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2])
    ga, gb = [], []
    for sig in [('A', (Z, Z)), ('none', (Z, Z)), ('A', (Z, Z))]:
        a, ra = G.step(a, sig, plastic=False); b, rb = G.step(b, sig, plastic=False)
        ga.append(ra['g']); gb.append(rb['g'])
    print(f'  equal depth but uneven within paths (weights by entry row ((1/4, 1/2), (3/4, 1/2)), both path sums 1, D = 0): R-uniform sets all four weights to 1/2 (the state changes; not the identity); '
          f'with fixed weights the gating records {ga} and {gb} are identical {ga == gb} — a positive example of reading equivalence; it does not make R-uniform a valid write-back')

def rd08():
    print('8. C₌-equivalent representation of the bifurcation reading: p̂(t) = −min_{s≤t} σ_s, ĝ(S) = −S (σ_s is the lower bound on the path sum at which the gating argument reaches threshold at beat s)')
    INF = None
    def sig_of(a, u, th):
        if u > 0:
            return (th - a) / u
        return '-inf' if a >= th else '+inf'
    def le(x, y):          # x ≤ y, x, y ∈ ℚ ∪ {±inf}
        if x == '-inf' or y == '+inf':
            return True
        if x == '+inf' or y == '-inf':
            return False
        return x <= y
    def mn(x, y):
        return x if le(x, y) else y
    # example with decreasing drive: λ = 1/2, θ = 10, history A, none, none
    G = gate(2, 2, {'A': (1, 1), 'none': (0, 0)}, F(1, 2), F(10), F(1, 2), F(1, 2), Z, Z, Z, F(1, 4), Z, F(2))
    us = [u_rho([1], F(1, 2), Z), u_rho([1, 0], F(1, 2), Z), u_rho([1, 0, 0], F(1, 2), Z)]
    print(f'  example: u_A in turn {[str(x) for x in us]} (decreasing); the constructed p̂ is defined by a prefix minimum and is nondecreasing by definition')
    rng = random.Random(31)
    n = viol = 0
    mono_fail = 0
    for trial in range(1500):
        lam = rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)])
        rho = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 3), F(1, 2), F(1)])
        plastic, bap = rng.choice([True, False]), rng.choice([True, False])
        Am = rng.choice([Z, F(1, 16)])
        th = rng.choice([F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)])
        def mk(theta):
            return GC.Gate(3, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0)}, [lam] * 2, [theta] * 2, [Z] * 2,
                           [F(1, 2)] * 3, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), Am, rho, [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(3)],
                           [[Z] * 2 for _ in range(3)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(3)])
        G, Gi = mk(th), mk(F(10 ** 6))
        S = [F(rng.randint(4, 40), 20), F(rng.randint(4, 40), 20)]
        if S[0] == S[1]:
            continue
        u0 = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 20)
        wb = F(rng.randint(0, 10), 20)
        w = [[S[0] / 2 + u0, S[1] / 2 + u0], [S[0] / 2 - u0, S[1] / 2 - u0], [wb, wb]]
        if not all(0 <= w[i][j] <= 2 for i in range(3) for j in range(2)):
            continue
        vv, yy = F(rng.randint(0, 5), 10), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)
        o = (w, [vv, vv], [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)] * 2 + [F(rng.randint(0, 3), 4)], [yy, yy])
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (lambda tv: (tv, tv))(rng.choice([Z, F(1, 4)]))) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 8))]
        # a run with the same parameters and no gating gives a(s), u(s); check that K has no clipping
        oi, clip, ab = [r[:] for r in o[0]], False, []
        oi = (oi, list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3]))
        for sig in hist:
            oi, ri = Gi.step(oi, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            clip = clip or any(ri['comp'][(i, j)][3] != ri['comp'][(i, j)][4] for i in (0, 1) for j in (0, 1))
            u = (ri['vt'][0] - ri['vt'][1]) / (S[0] - S[1])
            ab.append((ri['vt'][0] - u * S[0], u))
        if clip:
            continue
        sig_min, sigs = '+inf', []
        for (a_, u_) in ab:
            sig_min = mn(sig_min, sig_of(a_, u_, th))
            sigs.append(sig_min)
        mono_fail += any(not le(sigs[k + 1], sigs[k]) for k in range(len(sigs) - 1))
        # actual run: first gating beat of each output
        oo, tau = (o[0], list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3])), [None, None]
        for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
            oo, r = G.step(oo, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
            for j in (0, 1):
                if r['g'][j] and tau[j] is None:
                    tau[j] = t
        pred = [next((t for t, sg in enumerate(sigs) if le(sg, S[j])), None) for j in (0, 1)]
        n += 1
        viol += (pred != tau)
    print(f'  randomized check (SYM, ENT, no clipping in K; random ρ, plastic, bap), {n} cases: violations of "the prefix minimum of σ is nonincreasing" {mono_fail}; '
          f'mismatches of "first gating beat of output j = min{{t : p̂(t) ≥ ĝ(S_j)}}" {viol}')

def main():
    rd01()
    rd02()
    rd03()
    rd04()
    rd05()
    rd06()
    rd07()
    rd08()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

## Appendix B  Run Results

&nbsp;&nbsp;randomized check: 1276 cases with non-overlapping paths (differing baselines within a group included), only K changed, the rest of the start state the same, and no clipping (by switches and ρ\_eff: {'plastic/-/ρ\_eff\>0': 272, '-/bap/ρ\_eff=0': 308, 'plastic/-/ρ\_eff=0': 69, 'plastic/bap/ρ\_eff\>0': 233, '-/-/ρ\_eff=0': 329, 'plastic/bap/ρ\_eff=0': 65}; omitted because of clipping 55); cases where gating, v, x, y or the path sums disagree at some beat 0  
7\. Equal-depth identity, auxiliary operations and reading equivalence  
&nbsp;&nbsp;equal depth but uneven within paths (weights by entry row ((1/4, 1/2), (3/4, 1/2)), both path sums 1, D = 0): R-uniform sets all four weights to 1/2 (the state changes; not the identity); with fixed weights the gating records \[(1, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1)\] and \[(1, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1)\] are identical True — a positive example of reading equivalence; it does not make R-uniform a valid write-back  
8\. C₌-equivalent representation of the bifurcation reading: p̂(t) = −min\_{s≤t} σ\_s, ĝ(S) = −S (σ\_s is the lower bound on the path sum at which the gating argument reaches threshold at beat s)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;example: u\_A in turn \['1', '1/2', '1/4'\] (decreasing); the constructed p̂ is defined by a prefix minimum and is nondecreasing by definition  
&nbsp;&nbsp;randomized check (SYM, ENT, no clipping in K; random ρ, plastic, bap), 1245 cases: violations of "the prefix minimum of σ is nonincreasing" 0; mismatches of "first gating beat of output j = min{t : p̂(t) ≥ ĝ(S\_j)}" 0
